### [55] [Fluid Antenna-Aided Noise Modulation: Spatial Diversity for Variance-Based Wireless Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14958)
*Hadi Zayyani,Felipe A. P. de Figueiredo,Pedro M. R. Pereira,Fernando D. A. García,Rausley A. A. de Souza*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文将流体天线系统（FAS）与噪声调制（NoiseMod）相结合，解决了噪声调制在瑞利衰落信道下缺乏分集增益的问题。研究表明，流体天线能够恢复分集阶数，但该增益在天线孔径固定时会饱和，且存在一个由噪声样本数和方差比决定的内在误码率下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声调制（NoiseMod）通过在类噪声波形的方差而非幅度、相位或频率中编码信息，适用于超低功耗和隐蔽通信链路。然而，其主要弱点是在瑞利衰落信道下完全不具有分集特性：误码概率（BEP）仅以1/(N_sδ)的速度衰减，其中N_s为每比特噪声样本数，δ为有用信号与热噪声的方差比。与此同时，流体天线系统（FAS）已证明可以通过单个辐射元件在N_p个紧密间隔的端口间切换来恢复显著的选择分集，且无需额外的射频链路。因此，本文旨在将这两种技术结合，探索流体天线是否能克服噪声调制的分集缺陷。

Method: 本文采用理论分析与半解析方法相结合的研究路径：（1）对于理想化的相互独立端口情况，利用端口包络的顺序统计量推导了精确的闭式误码概率表达式；（2）对于由Jake模型支配的物理准确的空间相关情况，采用Khammassi等人的两阶段信道近似方法构建了半解析误码概率；（3）通过全信号级蒙特卡洛仿真对两种情况进行验证。

Result: 研究结果表明：（i）当端口弱相关时，FAS能够恢复随端口数N_p增长的分集阶数；（ii）当流体天线孔径Wλ固定而N_p增加时，该增益会饱和，这与FAS中已报道的 outage 概率饱和现象类似，但首次在误码概率中观察到；（iii）无论天线分集阶数如何，始终存在一个内在的、与相关性无关（且与δ无关）的误码率下限，该下限仅由N_s和方差比α决定。

Conclusion: 本文成功将流体天线系统应用于噪声调制接收机，证明了FAS能够有效恢复噪声调制在瑞利衰落下的分集增益。然而，分集增益受限于天线孔径，存在饱和效应。更重要的是，噪声调制存在一个固有的误码率下限，该下限无法通过增加天线分集来消除，这为噪声调制系统的性能极限提供了重要理论依据。

Abstract: Noise modulation (NoiseMod) encodes information in the \emph{variance} of a transmitted noise-like waveform rather than in its amplitude, phase, or frequency, and is attractive for ultra-low-power and covert links. Its main weakness is that, unlike classical modulation, it exhibits \emph{no} diversity under Rayleigh fading: its bit error probability (BEP) decays only as $1/(N_sδ)$, where $N_s$ is the number of noise samples per bit and $δ$ the useful-to-thermal noise variance ratio. Independently, fluid antenna systems (FAS) have been shown to recover substantial selection diversity from a single radiating element that switches among $N_p$ closely spaced ports, without extra radio-frequency chains. This paper combines the two: we equip a NoiseMod receiver with a fluid antenna and derive its average BEP. For idealized, mutually independent ports, we obtain an exact closed-form BEP via order statistics of the port envelopes. For the physically accurate, spatially correlated case governed by Jake's model, we build a semi-analytical BEP using the two-stage channel approximation of Khammassi \emph{et al.} We validate both regimes against full signal-level Monte Carlo simulation and show that (i) FAS restores a diversity order that grows with the number of ports $N_p$ when ports are weakly correlated, (ii) this gain saturates once the fluid-antenna aperture $Wλ$ is fixed and $N_p$ grows, mirroring the outage-probability saturation reported for FAS, now observed for BEP, and (iii) an intrinsic, correlation-independent (and $δ$-independent) BEP floor set only by $N_s$ and the variance ratio $α$ persists regardless of the antenna diversity order.

</details>


### [56] [The Minimal Measurement Number for Almost-Everywhere Complex Phase Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15003)
*Zhiqiang Xu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在复空间$\mathbb{C}^d$中，几乎处处相位恢复所需的最小测量次数恰好为$2d$，即当测量次数$m\leq 2d-1$时，强度测量映射无法唯一恢复几乎所有信号（相差一个全局相位因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相位恢复问题是信号处理、光学成像和量子态层析等领域中的核心问题，其目标是从强度测量（即丢失相位信息的测量）中恢复原始信号。已知$2d$个测量在一般情形下足以实现几乎处处相位恢复，但确定最小测量次数的精确下界仍是一个关键的理论问题。本文旨在证明$2d$不仅是充分条件，也是必要条件。

Method: 作者利用代数几何和测度论工具，分析强度测量映射$\mathcal{A}: \mathbb{C}^d/\mathbb{T} \to \mathbb{R}^m$的性质。通过研究测量映射在复射影空间上的纤维结构，证明当$m\leq 2d-1$时，该映射不是几乎处处单射的。具体而言，作者构造性地展示了在测量数不足时，存在正测度集的信号无法被唯一恢复。

Result: 主要定理：设$d\geq 2$，$\bm{f}_1,\ldots,\bm{f}_m\in\mathbb{C}^d$，若$m\leq 2d-1$，则强度测量映射在$\mathbb{C}^d$中几乎处处无法唯一恢复信号（允许相差一个全局相位因子$e^{i\theta}$）。结合已知的$2d$个测量在一般情形下充分的结论，完整确定了复空间中几乎处处相位恢复的最小测量数为$2d$。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了复空间$\mathbb{C}^d$中几乎处处相位恢复的最小测量数问题，确立了$2d$这一精确阈值。这一理论结果对相位恢复算法的采样复杂度分析具有重要指导意义，表明在复数情形下，相位恢复的内在信息论极限为$2d$个测量，这与实数情形（仅需$2d-1$个测量）形成对比，揭示了复结构带来的额外困难。

Abstract: Let $d\geq2$ and let $\bm{f}_1,\ldots,\bm{f}_m\in\mathbb C^d$. We prove that if \(m\leq 2d-1\), then the intensity measurement map fails to recover almost every signal in $\mathbb C^d$ uniquely up to a global phase factor. Combined with the known generic sufficiency of $2d$ measurements, our result establishes that the minimal number of measurements required for almost-everywhere phase retrieval in $\mathbb C^d$ is exactly $2d$.

</details>


### [57] [One-Shot Information Theory via the Pairwise Error Probability: Lossy, Joint Source-Channel, Erasure, and Multiuser Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15169)
*Nir Elkayam,Meir Feder*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文将基于成对错误概率(PEP)和错误谱的一次性信息论框架从点对点信道编码扩展到四个新场景（有损信源编码、联合信源信道编码、带删除选项的信道编码、多址信道），证明该统一原语可通过单一谱同时产生随机编码可达界和精确固定码恒等式，并支持线性规划先验优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在扩展一个基于单一原语（成对错误概率PEP及其诱导的错误谱）的一次性（有限块长）信息论框架。该框架在点对点信道编码中已展现出有效性（在配套论文中开发），作者希望证明这一统一原语可以推广到更广泛的场景，包括有损信源编码、联合信源信道编码、带删除/未检测错误选项的信道编码以及多用户多址信道，从而建立一个更加通用的有限块长分析工具。

Method: 核心方法是将基于成对错误概率（PEP）和错误谱的框架从点对点信道编码推广到更大的候选空间。具体技术包括：1）利用随机化、抖动破坏解码规则的PEP作为统一原语；2）通过错误谱表示可达性和逆界；3）将信道编码中的线性规划先验优化扩展到匹配解码下的新场景；4）针对每种设置建立单一的谱来同时得到随机编码可达界和精确固定码恒等式；5）对于多用户情况，通过三个成对错误事件连接可达界/逆界对。

Result: 成功证明了基于PEP和错误谱的统一框架可以涵盖四个新设置：1）平均失真下的有损信源编码；2）列表解码的联合信源信道编码；3）带删除/未检测错误选项的信道编码；4）两用户多址信道。在每个场景中，单一谱同时产生随机编码可达界和精确固定码恒等式。该工作恢复了Matsuta-Uyematsu的一次性有损界和Csiszar传统中的联合信源信道界，补充了Kostina-Verdu的有损界，并将多用户情况通过三个成对错误事件连接到可比较的可达/逆界对。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于成对错误概率和错误谱的原语具有显著的普适性，能够从点对点信道编码扩展到多种重要的信息论场景。该框架提供了一种统一的有限块长分析方法，通过线性规划先验优化和谱表示，在多个编码问题中同时实现可达界和精确恒等式，为有限块长信息论提供了一个强大而通用的数学工具。

Abstract: This paper extends a one-shot (finite-blocklength) information-theoretic framework built on a single primitive: the pairwise error probability (PEP) of a randomized, dither-broken decoding rule, and the error spectrum it induces. A companion paper developed the framework for point-to-point channel coding -- uniformity of the PEP, the error-spectrum representation of achievability and converse, and the linear-programming form of the prior-optimized minimax meta-converse. Here we show that the same primitive, read on an enlarged candidate space, governs four further settings: lossy source coding under average distortion, joint source-channel coding with list decoding, channel coding with an erasure/undetected-error option, and the two-user multiple-access channel. In each case a single spectrum yields a random-coding achievability bound and exact fixed-code identities, and we indicate how the convex -- indeed linear-programming -- prior optimization of the channel-coding case extends under matched decoding. The development recovers the one-shot lossy bound of Matsuta-Uyematsu and the joint source-channel bounds in the Csiszar tradition, complements the lossy bounds of Kostina-Verdu, and connects the multiuser case to the comparable achievability/converse pair through three pairwise error events.

</details>


### [58] [ICL-SEC: Iterative Cross-Layer Semantic Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15207)
*Yirun Wang,Soung Chang Liew,Yuyang Du*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代跨层语义纠错（ICL-SEC）框架，将信道译码器与语言模型通过迭代方式结合，利用"确认"机制逐步扩展可靠恢复的词集合，相比非迭代方案将误比特率降低两个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统迭代译码在信道编码中取得巨大成功，但现有语义通信缺乏物理层与应用层之间的迭代信息交互；非迭代的跨层语义纠错方案未能充分利用信道译码器与语言模型之间的相互增强潜力，存在性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出迭代跨层语义纠错（ICL-SEC）框架：（1）软输入软输出信道译码器输出比特级后验概率，推导词级可靠性；（2）可靠词作为语义上下文输入掩码语言模型，不可靠词被掩码；（3）语言模型输出上下文词似然，生成外源比特级先验反馈至信道译码器进行下一轮迭代；（4）核心贡献为"确认"先验更新规则——一旦词被判定为可靠，其比特在后续迭代中被赋予概率为1的确定性先验，成为完全确定的边信息，防止振荡性的解掩-掩码行为。

Result: 文本传输仿真表明ICL-SEC显著优于传统信道译码和非迭代CL-SEC；所提Confirm方案相对于非迭代CL-SEC将误比特率降低超过两个数量级，同时在其他五项性能指标上均有显著改善。

Conclusion: 通过将迭代译码原则引入语义纠错，ICL-SEC框架实现了物理层与应用层的闭环交互；"确认"机制确保了跨层可靠性解释的一致性，为语义通信系统设计提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Iterative decoding has been central to the success of modern channel coding, where reliability information is repeatedly exchanged across decoding components to approach fundamental performance limits. This paper brings the same principle to semantic error correction by proposing iterative cross-layer semantic error correction (ICL-SEC), a framework that closes the loop between physical-layer soft channel decoder and application-layer language-model-empowered semantic decoder. In the proposed framework, a soft-input soft-output channel decoder first produces bit-level posterior probabilities, from which word-level reliabilities are derived. Words deemed reliable are exposed to a masked language model as semantic context, while unreliable words are masked. The language model then produces contextual word likelihoods, which are leveraged to generate extrinsic bit-level priors and fed back to the channel decoder for the next iteration. This iterative refinement progressively expands the set of confidently recovered words. A key contribution is our Confirm prior-update rule: once a word is judged reliable, its bits are assigned deterministic priors with probability one in subsequent iterations, making the word fully resolved side information for both the channel decoder and the language model. This successive-confirmation mechanism prevents oscillatory unmask-mask behavior and yields a reliability interpretation consistent across layers. Simulations over text transmission demonstrates that ICL-SEC substantially outperforms both conventional channel decoding and non-iterative CL-SEC. In particular, the proposed Confirm scheme reduces the bit error rate by more than two orders of magnitude relative to non-iterative CL-SEC, while also significantly improving the other five performance metrics.

</details>


### [59] [Bringing Environmental Enhancement Back to Its Physical Essence via Specular Reflecting Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15271)
*Qingxiao Huang,Qianyao Ren,Yiqin Deng,Jun Huang,Yang Kun,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了机械驱动镜面反射面(SRS)在自由空间中的基本工作特性，建立了基于射线级联信道模型的分析框架，揭示了其在近场和远场的独特反射行为，并证明SRS在远场可能优于可重构智能表面(RIS)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能控制无线传播环境对未来网络容量和可靠性至关重要。与电路控制的可重构智能表面(RIS)相比，机械驱动的镜面反射面(SRS)提供了一种更简单且可能更具成本效益的替代方案，因此需要深入理解其基本工作原理和性能极限。

Method: 基于具有功率投影修正的可处理射线级联信道模型，采用以下分析方法：(1) 角度对齐近场分析，研究边缘反射引起的非建设性组合；(2) 远场行为分析；(3) 无界孔径渐近分析；(4) 小孔径和大孔径的近似分析（通过中心点和驻点分析）；(5) SRS波束方向图定义与3dB波束宽度推导；(6) 基于增益的近/远场边界定义；(7) SRS辅助通信系统可达速率的闭式推导。

Result: 主要发现包括：(1) 在角度对齐近场中，边缘反射导致非建设性组合，增益呈现阻尼振荡收敛至与孔径无关的常数；(2) 获得了远场行为、无界孔径渐近特性、最优孔径尺寸和反射角；(3) 定义了基于增益的近/远场边界；(4) 对于未对齐情况，提供了小孔径和大孔径的精确近似；(5) 定义了SRS波束方向图，推导了解析3dB波束宽度，量化了主瓣存在的有效区域；(6) 推导了SRS辅助通信系统的闭式可达速率；(7) 数值结果表明SRS在远场可以优于RIS，原因在于连续孔径和角度分辨率控制以及更强的功率投影。

Conclusion: 本文建立了SRS在自由空间中基本工作特性的完整理论框架，揭示了镜面反射与RIS不同的近/远场行为特性。研究表明，由于连续孔径和角度分辨率控制以及更强的功率投影，SRS在远场场景下具有超越RIS的潜力，为低成本智能无线环境控制提供了重要的理论基础和设计指导。

Abstract: Intelligent control of wireless propagation environments is crucial for future network capacity and reliability. Unlike circuit-controlled reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS), mechanically actuated specular reflecting surfaces (SRS) offer a simpler and potentially more cost-effective alternative. In this paper, based on the tractable ray-based cascaded channel model with power-projection correction, we investigate the fundamental operational behaviors of an ideal SRS in free space. Specifically, in the angle-aligned near field, edge reflections cause non-constructive combining, resulting in a damped oscillatory convergence of the gain to an aperture-independent constant. We further obtain the far-field behavior, unbounded-aperture asymptotics, an optimal aperture size and reflection angle, and a gain-based near/far-field boundary. For misalignment, we provide accurate approximations for small and large apertures via center-point and stationary-point analyses. We also define the SRS beam pattern, derive analytical 3-dB beamwidths, and quantify the effective region where a main lobe exists. Finally, we derive a closed-form achievable-rate for an SRS-aided communication system. Numerical results validate the proposed expressions, reveal distinct near-/far-field behaviors of specular reflection, and show that SRS can outperform RIS in the far field due to continuous aperture and angular-resolution control and stronger power projection.

</details>


### [60] [Study of Multiuser Scheduling Based on User Satisfaction for MU-MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15305)
*Ahmed M. Nor,Lukas T. N. Landau,E. Veronica Belmega,Rodrigo C. de Lamare*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 针对MU-MIMO系统中多用户调度的组合优化难题，提出基于用户满意度的低复杂度调度算法US-SA，通过构建低维子分组矩阵实现逐时隙最优调度，在保持用户公平性的同时接近穷举搜索的最优性能，且计算成本显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多用户多输入多输出(MU-MIMO)系统中的调度对于高效资源分配和整体性能提升至关重要。然而，在大量资源块(RB)中评估所有可能的多用户组合来最大化用户设备(UE)总体满意度的乘积（以保证用户公平性）是一个组合优化问题，穷举搜索(EX)的计算开销过大，难以实际应用。

Method: 提出了一种基于用户满意度的调度方法(US-SA)。该方法在每个帧构建一个低维子分组矩阵，用于在每个时隙调度最优的多用户组；已被满足的用户将从调度过程中移除，从而降低问题维度。

Result: US-SA在满意度、传输数据量、频谱效率、延迟和公平性方面接近最优的穷举搜索(EX)方法，同时计算成本更低。此外，实验表明所提方案优于现有竞争技术。

Conclusion: US-SA是一种高效的MU-MIMO多用户调度方案，通过低维子分组矩阵和逐时隙调度策略，在保证用户公平性的同时显著降低了计算复杂度，并在多项性能指标上接近最优性能。

Abstract: Scheduling in multiuser multiple input multiple output (MU-MIMO) systems is essential for efficient resource allocation and overall performance enhancement. In this work, a multiuser scheduling problem is formulated to maximize the product of user equipments' (UEs) aggregate satisfactions, which maintains user fairness. Solving such a combinatorial problem using exhaustive search (EX), which requires evaluating all possible multiuser groups within a massive number of resource blocks (RBs), is prohibitive. Instead, we propose an efficient users' satisfaction based scheduling approach (US-SA). In our US-SA, a low dimension sub-grouping matrix is constructed {at each frame}, which is used to schedule the best multiuser group in each time slot; satisfied users are eliminated from the scheduling process. Our US-SA performs close to the optimal EX method in terms of satisfaction, transmitted data amount, spectral efficiency, latency, and fairness with lower computational cost. Moreover, our experiments demonstrate that the proposed scheme outperforms competing techniques.

</details>


### [61] [New Approximations of Non-Separable MIMO Channels by Separable Channels for Accurate Ergodic Capacity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15427)
*Thanh Luan Nguyen,Zygmunt J. Haas,Chadi Abou-Rjeily,Georges Kaddoum*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对Weichselberger信道模型的不可分离结构导致的分析复杂性，提出了两种可分离近似方法：基于Kullback-Leibler散度的最近可分离模型和基于矩匹配的新方法，后者在全信噪比范围内均优于传统Kronecker模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weichselberger信道模型虽能精确刻画下一代无线应用中普遍存在的不可分离信道，但其不可分离结构带来了严重的分析复杂性，文献中缺乏可处理的数学框架，亟需解决该问题以推动MIMO系统研究。

Result: 1) KLD方法在稀疏和非规则散射环境中比传统Kronecker模型获得更紧致的容量估计，但在低信噪比下因无法保持总信道功率而存在容量表征偏差；2) MMM方法在全信噪比范围内一致性地改进了传统Kronecker模型的容量精度；3) 两种方法均能获得遍历容量的精确闭式表达式。

Conclusion: 本文提出的KLD和MMM方法有效解决了Weichselberger模型的分析复杂性问题，其中MMM方法作为更稳健的替代方案，在全信噪比范围内均优于传统Kronecker模型，为MIMO系统性能分析提供了可处理的数学框架。

Abstract: In recent years, owing to the high accuracy in characterizing non-separable channels prevalent in next-generation wireless applications, the classical Weichselberger channel model has gained widespread adoption in multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems. However, its non-separable structure also introduces severe analytical complexity, leading to a lack of tractable mathematical frameworks in the literature and thus raises an urgent need for further research. To address the aforementioned analytical complexity, we first derive the nearest separable (double-correlated Rayleigh) fading model to the Weichselberger model under the Kullback-Leibler divergence (KLD), a problem equivalent to rank-1 nonnegative matrix factorization under the Itakura-Saito (IS) distance criterion. The results of our asymptotic analysis in the high-SNR regime reveal that the KLD-enabled approximation achieves a tighter capacity estimate than the conventional Kronecker model, especially in sparse and non-regular scattering environments. Yet, a key limitation of the KLD-enabled model is its tendency to mischaracterize the channel capacity in the low-SNR regime due to its inability to preserve total channel power. As a more robust alternative, we introduce a novel moment matching method (MMM) aimed at mapping the exact channel statistics to those of a Wishart distribution. Both the KLD-enabled and MMM-enabled separable channel directly enable the use of exact closed-form expressions for the ergodic capacity. Numerical results demonstrate that the MMM-enabled model consistently improves upon the capacity accuracy of the conventional Kronecker model across all SNR regimes.

</details>

Method: 本文提出了一种互耦感知框架：（1）将期望波束幅度转换为适应有限孔径的复数目标；（2）将完整天线晶格建模为耦合多端口网络；（3）通过耦合电压-场响应选择有源端口及其源电压；其中未激活的候选端口仍作为网络的一部分承载感应电流，且所有对比设计均在相同电磁模型和相同源电压预算下进行评估。

Result: 数值结果表明，互耦感知设计相比互耦不感知选择和固定阵列，同时提升了平均主瓣信噪比（SNR）和峰值旁瓣电平（PSLL），证明互耦效应应在设计阶段本身加以利用，而非仅在最终评估中进行补偿。

Conclusion: 互耦效应不应被视为需要抑制或事后补偿的负面因素，而应作为设计的一部分主动加以利用；所提出的互耦感知框架能够有效提升流体天线系统的波束合成性能，为密集天线布局下的波束成形提供了更准确的物理模型和优化方法。

Abstract: Fluid antenna systems obtain spatial degrees of freedom by reconfiguring antenna positions within a confined region, a principle that extends to beamforming: shaped beams can be synthesized using far fewer radio-frequency feeds than candidate antenna positions. When the candidates are densely arranged, however, electromagnetic mutual coupling changes the relationship among terminal voltages, induced currents, and radiated fields, so an uncoupled model no longer describes the hardware and may activate an unsuitable set of ports, distorting the synthesized pattern. This paper develops a mutual-coupling-aware framework that converts the desired beam amplitude into a finite-aperture-compatible complex target and models the complete antenna lattice as a coupled multiport network, selecting the active ports and their source voltages through the coupled voltage-to-field response. Inactive candidate ports remain part of the network and carry induced currents, and every compared design is evaluated through the same electromagnetic model under the same source-voltage budget. Numerical results show that the mutual-coupling-aware design improves both the average mainlobe signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and the peak sidelobe level (PSLL) over coupling-unaware selection and a fixed array, demonstrating that mutual coupling should be exploited in the design itself rather than compensated only in the final evaluation.

</details>

Method: 本文提出了一种电磁感知波束成形框架，包含三个核心步骤：（1）利用相位恢复技术将仅幅度赋形波束指标转换为与孔径兼容的复数目标；（2）采用电磁感知正交匹配追踪（OMP）方法选择网格约束下的初始天线位置；（3）连续细化阶段交替执行精确电压约束电流优化和基于运动约束的投影自适应矩估计（Adam）更新所有物理天线位置。

Conclusion: 本文提出的电磁感知波束成形框架成功解决了流体天线阵列中几何优化与电磁耦合的联合问题。通过将相位恢复、稀疏选择与连续优化相结合，实现了在电压约束下的天线位置和激励电流的协同优化，为流体天线阵列的实际应用提供了有效的理论和方法支撑。

Abstract: Fluid antenna arrays exploit continuous antenna repositioning within a finite aperture to provide geometry diversity beyond grid-constrained port selection. Every displacement, however, changes both the radiation response and the multiport mutual-impedance network, coupling geometry optimization with the source-voltage constraint. This paper develops an electromagnetic-aware (EM-aware) beamforming framework for planar fluid antenna arrays. Phase retrieval converts an amplitude-only shaped-beam specification into an aperture-compatible complex target, and an EM-aware orthogonal matching pursuit (OMP) method selects grid-constrained initial antenna positions. Continuous refinement then alternates exact voltage-constrained current optimization with movement-constrained projected adaptive moment estimation (Adam) updates of all physical antenna positions. Across independently perturbed symmetric dual-beam targets, the proposed method consistently improves the average mainlobe signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and reduces the peak sidelobe level (PSLL) over a uniform array and discrete port selection.

</details>


### [64] [Time- and Space-Efficient List Decoding up to Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15937)
*Dorsa Fathollahi,Noga Ron-Zewi,Mary Wootters*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文构造了首个同时达到容量逼近、确定性近线性时间 ($N^{1+\tau}$) 和次线性空间 ($N^{\tau}$) 的列表可解码码，输出列表大小和字母表大小均为常数，并可扩展至列表可恢复码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在纠错码理论中，列表解码是一个核心问题：给定码 $C \subseteq \Sigma^N$ 和接收字 $y \in \Sigma^N$，找出所有满足 $\delta(c,y) \leq \rho$ 的码字 $c \in C$。虽然已有达到容量的列表可解码码及其快速近线性时间算法，但空间复杂度尚未得到充分研究。Cook 和 Moshkovitz (2024-2026) 开创了对低空间确定性算法的研究，但他们 2026 年的工作所构造的码远离容量。因此，需要构造同时满足时间效率、空间效率和容量逼近的列表可解码码。

Method: 本文提出了一种新的列表可解码码构造方法，实现了确定性的时间和空间高效列表解码算法。具体而言，对于任意速率 $R \in (0,1)$ 和任意小的常数 $\tau > 0$，构造了速率 $R$ 的码族，可以在时间 $N^{1+\tau}$ 和空间 $N^{\tau}$ 内确定性列表解码至半径 $\rho = 1 - R - \tau$。该方法可扩展至达到容量的列表可恢复码。

Result: 对于任意 $R \in (0,1)$ 和任意小的常数 $\tau > 0$，构造了满足以下性质的列表可解码码：
- 速率：$R$
- 列表解码半径：$\rho = 1 - R - \tau$（逼近容量）
- 解码时间：$N^{1+\tau}$（近线性时间）
- 解码空间：$N^{\tau}$（次线性空间）
- 输出列表大小：常数
- 字母表大小：常数

结果还可扩展至达到容量的列表可恢复码。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了同时逼近容量、具有确定性近线性时间算法和次线性空间复杂度的列表可解码码构造，填补了现有研究在时间效率与空间效率兼顾方面的空白。该成果对低空间复杂度纠错码算法的设计具有重要意义。

Abstract: In the theory of error correcting codes, list-decoding refers to the following problem. Given a code $C \subseteq Σ^N$ and a received word $y \in Σ^N$, find all codewords $c \in C$ so that $δ(c,y) \leq ρ$, where $δ$ is relative Hamming distance and $ρ\in (0,1)$. Codes that approach the optimal trade-off between the rate $R := \log_{|Σ|}(|C|) / N$ and the list-decoding radius $ρ$ are said to achieve capacity.By now, there are constructions of capacity-achieving list-decodable codes with fast near-linear-time list-decoding algorithms, but most existing work has not considered space complexity.
  In a recent line of work, Cook and Moshkovitz (2024, 2025, 2026) initiated the study of low-space deterministic algorithms for error correcting codes. In particular, in their 2026 paper, they gave a construction of list-decodable codes with deterministic near-linear-time and sublinear space list-decoding algorithms. However, these codes were far from achieving capacity.
  In this paper, we present list-decodable codes approaching capacity with deterministic time- and space-efficient list-decoding algorithms. More precisely, for any $R \in (0,1)$ and any arbitrarily small constant $τ> 0$, we present a family of codes $C\subseteq Σ^N$ with rate $R$ that are deterministically list-decodable up to radius $ρ= 1 - R - τ$, in time $N^{1 + τ}$ and space $N^τ$ with constant output list size and constant alphabet size. Our results can be extended to capacity-achieving list-recoverable codes.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无线边云推理场景中，分割学习(SL)需要将潜在表示从边缘设备传输至云端完成下游任务。传统方法对所有潜在分量采用同等保护，忽略了不同分量对下游任务的相关性差异，导致通信资源利用效率低下。作者旨在设计一种能够根据各潜在分量对下游任务的相关性动态调整传输可靠性的语义感知通信机制，从而提升系统整体推理性能。

Method: 1. 架构设计：构建基于自编码器(AE)的物理层，实现通信接口的端到端学习；2. 相关性量化：利用互信息(MI)估计各潜在分量与下游任务输出之间的统计依赖性，将MI估计值相对于各分量的梯度作为任务敏感性的代理指标；3. 非均匀差错保护(UEP)：基于MI驱动的优先级排序，在训练过程中对高相关性分量分配更高的保护等级；4. 物理层映射：将优先级转化为可测量的物理层效应，即对任务关键分量分配显著更高的发射功率。

Result: 1. 在真实物联网感知数据上的实验表明，所提方法在各种信噪比(SNR)条件下均一致优于均匀差错保护(EEP)和固定非均匀保护基线；2. MI引导的UEP将显著更高的发射功率分配给最关键的任务相关潜在分量；3. 附加分析验证了排序稳定性、估计器鲁棒性以及跨数据集和任务类型的泛化能力，表明该框架具有广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功建立了一种完全由学习驱动的任务感知语义分割学习框架，通过互信息梯度实现潜在分量的动态优先级排序，并将其有效映射至物理层资源分配。实验结果充分证明了该方法在边云协同事智能中的有效性和泛化能力，为语义通信和资源优化提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We propose a task-aware semantic split learning (SL) framework for wireless edge-cloud inference, in which the reliability of transmitted latent representations is dynamically adapted to their relevance for the downstream task. An autoencoder (AE)-based physical (PHY) layer enables end-to-end learning of the communication interface, while unequal error protection (UEP) is realized via mutual information (MI)-driven prioritization of latent components during training. The gradient of the estimated MI with respect to each latent component serves as a sensitivity-based proxy for task relevance, providing a fully learning-driven prioritization that adapts to both the data distribution and the downstream task. We further show that this prioritization translates into measurable physical-layer effects: MI-guided UEP assigns significantly higher transmit power to the most task-critical latent components compared to the equal error protection (EEP) baseline. Experiments on real-world IoT sensing data demonstrate consistent gains over equal and fixed-UEP baselines across SNR regimes. Additional analysis confirms ranking stability, estimator robustness and generalization across datasets and task types, indicating broad applicability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [66] [Maximal correlation under cardinality constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16397)
*Dror Drach,Tomer Berg,Or Ordentlich,Ofer Shayevitz*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文定义并分析了量化最大相关，通过MMSE失真和率失真技术获得可張量化的显式上界，进而得到乘积分布上的维度无关上界，并改进了可逆马尔可夫链等周常数的经典结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在扩展最大相关（maximal correlation）的概念，提出量化最大相关（quantized maximal correlation），即限制取值为有界基数集合的函数上的最大相关。现有最大相关理论在处理量化函数时存在局限，需要建立适用于有限取值集合的量化版本，以拓展其在信息论和概率论中的应用范围。

Method: 作者首先定义量化最大相关，并通过证明任意量化函数之间的相关性与特定随机变量线性组合量化中的MMSE（最小均方误差）失真相关，推导出量化最大相关的上界。随后，利用率失真（rate-distortion）技术和反集中不等式（anti-concentration inequalities）进一步约束该MMSE，从而获得量化最大相关的显式上界。关键技巧在于利用MMSE的張量化（tensorize）性质，克服量化最大相关本身不張量化的缺陷。

Result: 研究获得了量化最大相关的显式上界，该上界具有维度无关性（dimension-free），适用于乘积分布。这些结果进一步导出了可逆马尔可夫链和乘积链的等周常数（isoperimetric constants）的改进上界，强化了Alon和Milman等人的经典结果。

Conclusion: 该工作成功建立了量化最大相关的理论框架，通过MMSE和率失真技术获得了可張量化的显式上界，解决了原量化最大相关不張量化的问题。所得维度无关上界在乘积分布上具有重要应用，并在马尔可夫链等周问题中取得对经典结果的改进，展示了量化最大相关在信息论、概率论和组合数学中的广泛理论价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we define and analyze the quantized maximal correlation, an extension of the notion of maximal correlation restricted to functions taking values in sets of bounded cardinality. We derive an upper bound on the quantized maximal correlation by showing that the correlation between any quantized functions of $X$ and $Y$ is related to the MMSE distortion in quantization of a particular linear combination of random variables. Following this, we leverage rate-distortion techniques and anti-concentration inequalities to further bound this MMSE, which results in explicit bounds on the quantized maximal correlation. Unlike the quantized maximal correlation itself, which does not generally tensorize, our bounds on the mean squared error do tensorize, resulting in a dimension-free upper bound on the quantized maximal correlation for product distributions. Our results also lead to improved bounds on the isoperimetric constants of reversible Markov chains and product chains, strengthening classical results such as those by Alon and Milman.

</details>


### [67] [Revisiting Shannon's Source Coding Theorem with Distributional Uncertainty under the Nonlinear Expectation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16543)
*Wen-Xuan Lang,Shaoshi Yang,Jianhua Zhang,Zhiming Ma*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文基于非线性期望理论，将经典信息熵推广为非线性信息熵，建立了不确定分布信源的非线性信源编码定理，证明了非线性信息熵刻画了此类信源的编码率极限，扩展了经典信息论至分布不确定的复杂场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息论中假设信源由单一、精确已知的概率分布建模，但实际复杂通信网络中存在大量不可预期、非平稳、异质的随机事件，确定性概率模型的假设过于理想化。因此，需要放松确定性概率模型的假设，刻画信源消息分布本身的不确定性。

Method: 基于非线性期望理论（经典概率论的新公理化扩展），研究分布本身不确定的信源（称为不确定分布信源）；将信息熵推广为非线性信息熵，用于度量不确定分布信源所含信息量；利用次线性期望下的强大数定律，建立非线性信源编码定理。

Result: 1）非线性信息熵是不确定分布信源在最大错误概率准则下可达编码率下确界的上界；2）确定了不确定分布信源在最小错误概率准则下编码率的一个聚点；3）非线性期望理论的引入为信息源提供了更全面的理解框架。

Conclusion: 通过引入非线性期望理论，本文成功将经典信息论扩展至分布本身不确定的场景，建立了非线性信源编码定理，证明了非线性信息熵在编码问题中的基本极限作用，为复杂网络环境下的信息理论分析提供了新的数学工具和理论框架。

Abstract: In classical information theory, a source is modeled by a single, precisely known probability distribution. However, in the increasingly complex communication networks full of unanticipated, nonstationary, and heterogeneous random events, the assumption of precise and well-defined probability distributions to describe random variables appears somewhat idealized. Therefore, it is important to characterize the uncertainty of distributions of source messages, subject to relaxing the assumption of deterministic probability models for analyzing information sources in information theory. Based on the nonlinear expectation theory, a novel axiomatical system that extends classical probability theory, this paper investigates the information sources whose distributions themselves are uncertain, and refers to them as uncertain-distribution sources. We generalize the fundamental concept information entropy to nonlinear information entropy, which describes the measurement of the amount of information contained in a uncertain-distribution source. By using the strong law of large numbers under sublinear expectation, we establish a nonlinear source coding theorem, which not only shows that the nonlinear information entropy is the upper bound for the infimum of achievable coding rate of uncertain-distribution sources under the maximum error probability criterion, but also determines a cluster point of the coding rate of uncertain-distribution sources under the minimum error probability criterion. Our findings reveal that the introduction of nonlinear expectation theory allows for a more comprehensive understanding of information sources.

</details>

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出FIM增强的无蜂窝通感一体化架构，通过分布式AP的多角度观测，将FIM形态增益从0.4 dB提升至15.8 dB，并设计了联合优化算法实现4.5 dB定位精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体智能超表面（FIM）是一种新兴的天线架构，通过连续改变物理几何形状来优化无线性能。然而，现有FIM辅助的通感一体化（ISAC）研究依赖于共址单基站部署，由于观测角度受限，未能充分利用FIM的形态灵活性。因此，需要探索分布式架构以释放FIM的潜力。

Method: 本文研究了FIM增强的无蜂窝ISAC架构，其中分布式接入点（AP）从多个角度协同观测目标。研究推导了目标角度估计的完整Fisher信息矩阵，获得了显式量化角度多样性增益的闭式定位CRB。通过分析Fisher信息矩阵的块结构，发现了三个无蜂窝特有的现象。进一步提出了基于半定松弛的联合波束成形和FIM形状设计的交替优化算法，并形式化证明了其紧性。

Result: 在28 GHz配置下，四个AP、每个AP八个FIM元件的分析表明，分布式角度多样性将FIM形态增益放大至15.8 dB，而相同总天线数的单AP对部署仅为0.4 dB。所提无蜂窝FIM-ISAC架构在10 dB感知SNR下比单AP固定阵列基线实现了4.5 dB的定位CRB降低，同时在整个Pareto前沿保持通信服务质量约束。

Conclusion: 分布式无蜂窝架构能够显著放大FIM的形态灵活性增益，通过多角度观测克服单基站部署的角度限制。所提出的联合优化算法有效实现了通感一体化性能提升，为FIM在实际无线系统中的应用提供了重要理论基础和设计指导。

Abstract: Fluid intelligent metasurface (FIM) is an emerging antenna architecture that continuously reshapes its physical geometry to optimize wireless performance. While existing studies on FIM-aided integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) rely on co-located single-base-station (BS) deployments, they fundamentally underutilize FIM's morphological flexibility due to restricted observation angles. In this paper, we investigate a FIM-augmented cell-free ISAC architecture, where distributed access points (APs) collaboratively observe a target from diverse angles. We derive the complete Fisher information matrix for target angle estimation and obtain a closed-form localization CRB that explicitly quantifies the angular diversity gain. By analyzing the block structure of the Fisher information matrix, we uncover three cell-free-specific phenomena: (i) cross-AP information coupling, (ii) multiplicative Tx--Rx FIM coupling, and (iii) angular diversity amplification. Under a 28\,GHz configuration with four APs and eight FIM elements per AP, our analysis shows that distributed angular diversity amplifies the FIM morphing gain to 15.8\,dB, compared to only 0.4\,dB in a single-AP pair deployment with the same total antenna count. We further propose an alternating optimization algorithm for joint beamforming and FIM shape design via semidefinite relaxation whose tightness is formally proved. Numerical results confirm that the proposed cell-free FIM-ISAC architecture achieves a 4.5\,dB localization CRB reduction over the single-AP fixed-array baseline at 10\,dB sensing SNR while maintaining communication quality-of-service constraints across the entire Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [69] [Two-Level Decorrelated Coded Modulation on the $D_4$ Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16662)
*Leopold Bertholet,Chloe Makdad,Stephen Mackes,Daniel Chew,Matthew Robinso*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TLDC的新型编码调制方案，针对D4格点结合Voronoi成形和两级译码，在16-QAM AWGN信道下比现有方案增益达0.5 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有格点编码调制方案在实现格点成形和编码增益时复杂度较高，需要一种低复杂度的方案来同时获得成形增益和编码增益。

Method: 提出两级去相关编码(TLDC)：1) 针对D4格点设计，结合Voronoi成形；2) 采用两级译码结构，第一级译码结果使第二级的多个随机变量近似去相关；3) 第二级变量的独立性支持并行译码或合并为更大码字；4) 每级可灵活选择FEC方案（如比特交织极化码或多级极化码）。

Result: 在AWGN信道下，基于16-QAM的TLDC方案在码块大小为64和1024比特时，相比同类先进编码调制方案可获得高达0.5 dB的增益。

Conclusion: TLDC通过两级去相关结构有效降低了格点编码调制的复杂度，同时保持灵活的前向纠错选择，在短码块场景下展现出显著的性能优势。

Abstract: We propose \textit{two-level decorrelated coding} (TLDC), a novel coded modulation scheme for the $D_4$ lattice that combines Voronoi shaping with a two-stage decoding process to achieve lattice shaping and coding gains at low complexity. In TLDC, the decoded values of the first level allow the several random variables in the second level to become approximately uncorrelated. The resulting independence of the variables in level two permits decoding in parallel or consolidation into a larger codeword, enhancing performance. TLDC supports flexible choice of FEC within each level. Using bit-interleaved or multi-level polar codes at each level, the resulting coded modulation scheme exhibits a gain of up to 0.5 dB over analogous state-of-the-art coded modulation schemes on a 16-QAM under AWGN at block sizes of 64 and 1024 bits.

</details>

*Pavan Kumar,Shayan Srinivasa Garani*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文构造了两类具有精确码率的非二进制纠缠辅助准循环量子LDPC码，分别基于一对和单个经典QC-LDPC码设计，通过图论方法消除4-环并最小化纠缠资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码是实现可靠量子通信和量子计算的关键技术。非二进制量子LDPC码相比二进制码具有更好的纠错性能和更高的编码效率，但现有研究主要集中在二进制情形。此外，纠缠辅助量子纠错码可以利用预共享纠缠资源降低编码约束，提高码率，但如何有效平衡纠错性能与纠缠消耗仍是一个重要问题。准循环结构便于硬件实现，因此构造高效的非二进制纠缠辅助准循环量子LDPC码具有重要的理论和应用价值。

Method: 本文提出了两种构造方法：第一类方法基于一对非二进制经典QC-LDPC码进行构造，确保所得EA-QC-QLDPC码的非辅助部分的Tanner图无4-环，且仅使用单个Bell对建立收发端之间的纠缠；第二类方法基于单个非二进制经典QC-LDPC码构造，要求其Tanner图本身无4-环。两种方法均通过精心的图论设计来控制码的代数结构和组合特性。

Result: 成功构造了两类具有精确确定码率的非二进制纠缠辅助准循环量子LDPC码：第一类码通过最小化纠缠资源（仅使用单个Bell对）实现了非辅助部分Tanner图的无4-环特性；第二类码基于单个经典码实现了整体Tanner图的无4-环特性。这些构造证明了基于图的精心设计能够有效平衡纠错性能与纠缠消耗，为实际实现高效的非二进制EA-QC-QLDPC码提供了可行途径。

Conclusion: 本文通过两种系统性的构造方法，成功设计了一类能够精确控制码率、最小化纠缠资源且具有良好的图论性质（无4-环）的非二进制纠缠辅助准循环量子LDPC码。研究表明，图论设计在优化量子纠错码的性能与资源消耗权衡中起着关键作用，为未来量子通信系统中实用化的高效量子纠错码设计提供了理论基础和技术路径。

Abstract: We construct two families of non-binary entanglement assisted (EA) quasi-cyclic (QC) quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes over arbitrary finite fields, each possessing a precisely determined code rate.
  The first family is derived from a pair of non-binary classical QC-LDPC codes, designed such that the unassisted portion of the overall Tanner graph of the resulting EA-QC-QLDPC code is free of 4-cycles. The second family, on the other hand, is constructed from a single non-binary classical QC-LDPC code whose Tanner graph itself is 4-cycle-free. In developing the codes belonging to the first family, we employ a \emph{single Bell pair} to establish entanglement between the transmitter and the receiver, thereby minimizing the required entanglement resources. Furthermore, these constructions demonstrate that careful graph-based design can effectively balance error-correction performance with entanglement consumption, providing a practical approach for realizing efficient non-binary EA-QC-QLDPC codes.

</details>


### [71] [Fluid Antenna Array-Inspired Location-Posterior-Driven Subarray Sizing and Power Control for Two-Hop AF UAV Relaying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16770)
*Xuanyi Zhu,Jian Dang,Chen Zhao,Huaifeng Shi,Zaichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受流体天线阵列启发的子阵尺寸与发射功率联合设计方法，用于两跳放大转发无人机中继系统。通过利用可收缩的用户位置后验信息，自适应调整可重构子阵的有效孔径，在阵列增益与指向鲁棒性之间取得平衡，从而在满足最差用户速率要求和硬件功率限制的前提下最小化无人机功耗。仿真表明，所提方法相比全阵列窄波束传输可节省3.17 dB功耗，并在0.15 W预算下实现60%的服务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机中继通信中，用户位置存在不确定性，传统固定阵列设计难以在阵列增益（需要窄波束/大孔径）与指向鲁棒性（需要宽波束/小孔径）之间取得最优平衡。此外，两跳AF中继中子阵尺寸同时影响接收增益、转发增益和波束宽度，需要联合优化。因此，需要一种能够根据用户位置不确定性自适应调整子阵几何结构的设计方法，以降低功耗并保证服务质量。

Method: 1. 采用流体天线阵列(FAA)启发的可重构子阵架构，使连续可重构子阵被两跳链路时分共享；2. 利用逐渐收缩的用户位置后验分布来跟踪位置不确定性；3. 将位置协方差投影到阵列方向，导出与方向不确定性成反比的闭式方向受限子阵尺寸；4. 通过后验样本传播计算两跳速率模型；5. 联合优化子阵尺寸与发射功率，以最小化无人机功耗，约束条件包括最差用户速率下尾要求和硬件功率限制；6. 在真实用户位置处的瞬时两跳Rician信道上对规划配置进行审计验证。

Result: 1. 在t=8 s时刻，所提设计在配对可行几何结构上相比全阵列窄波束传输节省3.17 dB功耗；2. 在0.15 W功率预算下，所提方法实现60.0%的服务成功率，而固定八单元子阵仅为43.2%；3. 通过自适应控制有效孔径，成功利用几何可重构性在阵列增益与指向鲁棒性之间取得平衡；4. 方向受限子阵尺寸与方向不确定性成反比的闭式解有效降低了优化复杂度；5. 所提渐进收缩后验的方法能够有效处理用户位置不确定性，保证两跳AF中继的端到端速率性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于流体天线阵列启发的自适应子阵尺寸与功率联合设计方法，通过将位置不确定性投影到阵列方向并导出闭式子阵尺寸，有效解决了两跳AF无人机中继中阵列增益与指向鲁棒性的权衡问题。该方法以较低复杂度实现了显著的功耗节省和更高的服务成功率，为位置不确定场景下的可重构阵列无人机中继通信提供了有效解决方案。渐进收缩后验的框架具有良好的扩展性，可进一步推广到多用户、多无人机协作等更复杂场景。

Abstract: This paper develops fluid antenna array (FAA)-inspired subarray sizing and transmit-power design for a two-hop amplify-and-forward (AF) unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) relay using progressively contracting user-location posteriors. A contiguous reconfigurable subarray is shared by first-hop reception and second-hop forwarding, such that its active size jointly determines the receive gain, forwarding gain, and beamwidth. By adaptively controlling the effective aperture, the proposed design exploits geometric reconfigurability to balance array gain against pointing robustness under location uncertainty. Projecting the position covariance onto the array direction yields a closed-form direction-limited size inversely proportional to directional uncertainty. Posterior samples are propagated through the two-hop rate model, and the subarray size and transmit power are then selected to minimize UAV power subject to a worst-user lower-tail rate requirement and hardware power limits. The planned configuration is further audited over instantaneous two-hop Rician channels at the true user positions. At t = 8 s, the proposed design saves 3.17 dB over full-array narrow-beam transmission on paired feasible geometries and achieves 60.0% service success at a 0.15-W budget, compared with 43.2% for a fixed eight-element subarray.

</details>


### [72] [Sample Complexity of Peer Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16838)
*Abdellah Aznag,Robin Bowers,Rachel Cummings,Jason Hartline,Matthew vonAllmen,Bo Waggoner*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文刻画了peer prediction中不同样本数量下具有无偏估计器的互信息集合：3个及以下样本时仅有平凡零解，4-5个二元样本时行列式互信息(DMI)唯一，6个样本时唯一性消失；并提出改进的DMI估计器和随机样本数量的估计策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Peer prediction旨在通过奖励联合报告集合来激励智能体真实报告观测信号，而无需观测真实值。现有研究缺乏对具有无偏估计器的互信息集合的系统刻画，特别是针对不同样本数量的情况。本文旨在刻画在给定样本数量下具有无偏估计器的互信息集合。

Method: 本文基于Kong和Schoenebeck (2019)引入的信息论互信息的推广，定义了互信息为联合分布的非负函数且对所有信息结构都能抑制报告混淆。研究采用理论分析方法：1) 分析3个及以下样本对时互信息的性质；2) 分析4-5个二元报告空间样本时的唯一性；3) 证明6个样本时DMI不再唯一；4) 提出改进的DMI估计器并分析其收敛率；5) 研究随机样本数量的互信息估计器，包括"stop-short估计器"和基于评分规则的互信息族。

Result: 主要理论结果包括：1) 对于3个及以下采样报告对，唯一具有无偏估计器的互信息是平凡零函数；2) 对于4-5个样本且二元报告空间，行列式互信息(DMI)是唯一的互信息（至多相差标量倍数）；3) DMI在6个样本时不再具有唯一性；4) 提出了改进的DMI估计器并刻画了其收敛率；5) 证明了"stop-short估计器"可以比固定样本估计器获得更低方差；6) 在基于评分规则的互信息族中识别出一种期望样本数低于3即可估计的互信息。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了不同样本数量下具有无偏估计器的互信息集合，发现样本数量对互信息的唯一性有决定性影响：3个及以下样本时只有平凡解，4-5个二元样本时DMI唯一，6个样本时唯一性消失。此外，随机化样本数量（特别是stop-short策略）可以降低估计方差，而基于评分规则的互信息族能够在期望样本数低于3时实现估计，为peer prediction机制设计提供了重要的理论基础。

Abstract: Peer prediction seeks to incentivize agents to truthfully report an observed signal by rewarding joint sets of reports without observing a ground truth. Following the generalization of information-theoretic mutual information introduced in Kong and Schoenebeck (2019), we call a function of a joint distribution over signals a mutual information when it is non-negative and disincentivizes garbling reports for all information structures. An unbiased estimator for a mutual information takes some number of samples from the distribution and returns rewards for both agents, such that the expected reward is equal to the mutual information. We seek to characterize the set of mutual informations with unbiased estimators for a given number of samples.
  We show that for three or fewer sampled report pairs, the only mutual information with an unbiased estimator is trivially zero, and for four or five samples with a binary report space, the Determinant Mutual Information (DMI) of Kong (2024) is the unique mutual information (up to a scalar multiple). We further show that DMI ceases to be unique at six samples. We provide an improved estimator of DMI for any given number of samples and characterize its convergence rate.
  We also examine mutual information estimators that accept a randomized number of samples. First, we show that mutual information estimators on an ex-ante bounded number of samples (termed "stop-short estimators") can achieve a lower variance than an equivalent fixed-sample estimator (for DMI). Second, we introduce the class of scoring-rule-based mutual informations and identify in this family a mutual information that can be estimated with under three samples in expectation.

</details>
