### [73] [Persistent Spatio-Temporal Outage Hotspot Detection for Infrastructure Resilience Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14572)
*Solongo Ganbold,Sohini Roy*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文构建了融合热点持续性指数（HPI）与改进蕴含性相互依赖模型（MIIM）的数据驱动地理空间框架，识别美国电网中反复出现的停电脆弱区域，发现三个持续性区域集群贡献过半级联影响，通信层失效更为严重，且HPI引导的加固策略优于纯拓扑方法，为基础设施韧性规划提供了经验驱动的决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端天气事件在美国造成了持续性的电网中断地理格局，但停电热点检测与基础设施级联建模通常被分开研究，缺乏将二者关联的系统性框架。同时，现有方法难以识别反复出现的停电脆弱区域，且基于拓扑的韧性规划可能忽略实际地理风险模式。

Method: 本研究提出了一种数据驱动的地理空间框架，整合持续性停电脆弱性与下游级联影响分析。具体包括：（1）基于2015-2023年全国停电数据集，构建热点持续性指数（HPI），量化县级停电热点的反复出现程度与严重性；（2）采用多尺度DBSCAN精化方法，将县级热点转化为具有复发性、严重性和空间范围特征的区域性故障情景；（3）利用改进的蕴含性相互依赖模型（MIIM），将经验导出的故障情景注入耦合的电力-通信网络层，模拟级联失效行为；（4）基于HPI结果指导网络加固策略，并与度中心性和介数中心性基准进行对比评估。

Result: 研究发现三个持续性区域集群贡献了54.4%的HPI加权级联总影响；在高持续性情景下，通信层实体失效速率为电力母线的2.5倍；HPI引导的加固策略重新优化了保护候选节点的优先级，能够识别出仅依赖拓扑排序方法所遗漏的高价值母线。这些发现验证了持续性地理空间停电模式对针对性、经验驱动型基础设施韧性规划的支持作用。

Conclusion: 该研究通过HPI指标与MIIM级联模型的耦合，建立了从地理空间停电模式识别到跨层基础设施级联评估的完整分析链条，证明将经验性地理脆弱性数据与网络相互依赖建模相结合，可显著提升电力-通信网络韧性规划的科学性与针对性。

Abstract: Extreme weather events are producing persistent geographic patterns of power-grid disruption across the United States, yet outage hotspot detection and infrastructure cascade modeling are often studied separately. This paper presents a data-driven geospatial framework that links persistent outage vulnerability with downstream cascade impact in interdependent power-communication networks. Using a national outage dataset from 2015-2023, we introduce the Hotspot Persistence Index (HPI), a severity-aware metric for identifying counties that repeatedly emerge as outage hotspots over time. We then apply a multi-scale DBSCAN refinement procedure to convert persistent county-level hotspots into geographically interpretable regional failure scenarios characterized by recurrence, severity, and spatial extent. To evaluate their system-level relevance, these empirically derived scenarios are injected into the Modified Implicative Interdependency Model (MIIM), which captures cascading behavior across coupled power and communication layers. Results show that three persistent regional clusters account for 54.4% of total HPI-weighted cascade impact, while communication-layer entities fail at 2.5X the rate of power buses under high-persistence scenarios. HPI-guided hardening reprioritizes protection candidates relative to a degree- and betweenness-centrality baseline, identifying high-value buses that topology-only rankings overlook. These results demonstrate how persistent geospatial outage patterns can support targeted and empirically grounded infrastructure resilience planning.

</details>


### [74] [WARA: Toward Automated Wireless Optimization Research with Closed-Loop LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14573)
*Yuan Guo,Yilong Chen,Chao Hu,Xianghao Yu,Liang Hong,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向无线资源分配优化的端到端自动研究框架WARA，通过闭环多智能体系统和制品介导控制实现研究全流程自动化，产出质量接近同行评审论文水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）智能体在工具使用、代码执行、制品检查和迭代修正方面的能力日益增强，为科学和工程研究的自动化创造了新机遇。然而，在无线通信领域，尚缺乏端到端的自动化研究框架，特别是在无线资源分配优化这一核心方向上。因此，需要构建一个能够自主完成从问题提出到成果产出的完整研究流程的系统。

Method: 本文提出了无线自动研究智能体（WARA），一个面向无线优化研究的闭环多智能体系统。WARA将研究工作流分解为三个阶段：（1）研究空白识别与问题提出；（2）无线优化建模、算法设计与实验；（3）研究成果构建。系统采用制品介导控制机制：上游制品作为下游输入，结构化输出存储供后续使用，控制器管理的门控机制验证模型、算法、实验与声明之间的一致性。当验证失败时，WARA仅修复负责的制品而非重启整个工作流。此外，设计了基于LLM的ScoringAgent来评估稿件级研究有效性和优化研究成熟度。

Result: 通过代表性的无线资源分配案例研究，展示了WARA如何将初始主题转化为包含可执行证据和综合技术稿件的完整研究包。对比实验表明，WARA显著优于单次LLM生成，且其质量特征接近近期被接收的同行评审技术论文。闭环制品控制被证明是实现端到端LLM辅助无线优化研究的有前景路径。

Conclusion: WARA作为首个面向无线领域的端到端自主研究框架，通过闭环多智能体架构和制品介导控制，实现了无线资源分配优化研究的自动化。该系统在保持各组件一致性的同时，能够迭代修复错误而不必重启全流程，其产出质量已接近同行评审论文水平，为LLM辅助科学研究提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly capable of tool use, code execution, artifact inspection, and iterative revision, creating new opportunities for automating scientific and engineering research. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first end-to-end autoresearch framework for the wireless domain, with a focus on wireless resource allocation optimization. We propose the Wireless AutoResearch Agent (WARA), a closed-loop multi-agent system for automated wireless optimization research. Given only an initial topic, WARA decomposes the workflow into three phases: research gap identification and problem proposal, wireless optimization modeling, algorithm design and experimentation, and research deliverable construction. Across these phases, WARA uses artifact-mediated control: upstream artifacts are consumed as inputs, structured outputs are stored for downstream use, and controller-managed gates validate consistency among models, algorithms, experiments, and claims. When validation fails, WARA repairs only the responsible artifact instead of restarting the whole workflow. We present a representative wireless resource allocation case study showing how WARA converts an initial topic into a complete research package with executable evidence and a synthesized technical manuscript. We further design a structured LLM-based ScoringAgent to evaluate manuscript-level research validity and optimization research maturity. Comparative results show that WARA substantially outperforms one-shot LLM generation and approaches the quality profile of recently accepted peer-reviewed technical papers. These results indicate that closed-loop artifact control is a promising path toward end-to-end LLM-assisted wireless optimization research. The source code is available at https://github.com/guoyuan-dotcom/WARA_CUHKSZ.

</details>


### [75] [From Reactive to Autonomous: Evolution of AI Operations in Cloud Network Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14574)
*Arun Malik*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文基于超大规模网络基础设施的生产运营经验，提出了云网络AIOps的五代成熟度模型，指出从被动运维到自主运维的演进不仅是技术问题，更需要工具、信任框架、知识管理和运营文化的协同演化，并为基础设施组织提供了渐进式采用自主AI运维的实用路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云网络基础设施的运维模式在过去十年经历了根本性变革，从人工驱动的问题排查逐步演进到完全自主的事件解决。然而，这一演进过程中存在架构模式、组织挑战和技术拐点等关键问题尚未被系统梳理，且基础设施组织缺乏明确的指导框架来实现向更高自主性的过渡。

Method: 基于超大规模网络基础设施的生产运营经验，采用归纳分析法：1) 追溯AIOps在云网络中的演进历程；2) 识别各代际转换中的架构模式、组织挑战和技术拐点；3) 构建五代成熟度模型；4) 分析代际转换的技术与组织障碍；5) 定义指示自主性就绪度的关键指标。

Result: 提出了包含五个不同运营代际的AIOps成熟度模型；识别了阻碍代际转换的技术和组织壁垒；确定了标志自主性就绪度的关键指标；论证了从被动运维到自主运维的演进需要工具、信任框架、知识管理实践和运营文化的协同演化；为基础设施组织提供了渐进式采用自主AI运维的实用路线图。

Conclusion: 云网络基础设施从被动运维到自主运维的转型本质上是一个涉及技术、组织和文化多维度的协同演化过程。单一的技术升级不足以支撑代际跃迁，必须同步构建可信的AI决策框架、系统化的知识管理体系以及支持自主运营的组织文化。该成熟度模型和路线图可为不同发展阶段的基础设施组织提供可操作的演进参考。

Abstract: The operational model for cloud network infrastructure has undergone a fundamental transformation over the past decade. What began as manual, human-driven troubleshooting has evolved through scripted automation, rule-based systems, and AI-assisted operations into fully autonomous incident resolution. This paper traces the evolution of AI operations (AIOps) in cloud network infrastructure, identifying the architectural patterns, organizational challenges, and technical inflection points that enabled each generational transition. Drawing from production experience operating network infrastructure at hyperscale, we present a maturity model that characterizes five distinct operational generations, analyze the technical and organizational barriers that impede transitions between generations, and document the metrics that indicate readiness for increased autonomy. We show that the path from reactive to autonomous operations is not merely a technology problem but requires co-evolution of tooling, trust frameworks, knowledge management practices, and operational culture. Our findings provide a practical roadmap for infrastructure organizations seeking to adopt progressively autonomous AI operations.

</details>


### [76] [HW-Router: Hardware-Aware Routing for Scalable Multi-LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14575)
*Ahasan Kabir,Jiaqi Xue,Mengxin Zheng,Qian Lou*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出HW-Router，一种动态路由框架，通过整合实时硬件信号（队列长度、KV缓存利用率、TTFT/TPOT等）进行准确的延迟预测和SLO感知路由，解决了现有基于静态模型属性的路由方法在多LLM服务场景中的次优决策问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务平台的路由方法主要依赖静态模型属性（如模型大小、FLOPs）来估计服务成本，无法捕捉真实部署中的动态行为。相同模型在不同硬件（H100 vs V100）、系统负载（运行和等待队列长度）和资源竞争（KV缓存使用、GPU利用率）下表现出显著不同的推理延迟。这种硬件无关的路由导致SLO违规、队列堆积和GPU利用率低下等问题。

Method: 提出HW-Router动态路由框架，核心方法包括：(1) 整合模型特定特征（架构、大小、输入长度）与硬件指标（队列长度、KV缓存利用率、近期TTFT/TPOT性能）；(2) 使用轻量级延迟预测器估计每个模型-每个GPU的服务时间；(3) 基于实时硬件信号进行SLO感知的智能路由决策。

Result: 在多样化工作负载上的评估显示，与SOTA基线CARROT和IRT相比，HW-Router实现了：端到端延迟降低3.4-3.9倍、SLO达成率提高46-48个百分点、GPU负载偏斜降低6-8倍、等待队列比例减少3.1-3.4倍，仅增加约200μs的路由开销且输出质量无损失。

Conclusion: 实时硬件反馈对于可扩展、可预测且负载均衡的多LLM服务至关重要。HW-Router通过将硬件信号整合到模型选择中，显著提升了服务效率和系统性能。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) serving platforms deploy multiple models across different GPUs, requiring routers to direct incoming queries to appropriate LLMs. However, existing routing approaches primarily rely on static model attributes such as size or FLOPs to estimate serving costs. This static cost modeling fails to capture the dynamic behavior of real deployments, where the same model can exhibit vastly different inference latencies depending on hardware type (e.g., H100 vs. V100), current system load (e.g., running and waiting queue lengths), and resource contention (e.g., KV-cache usage and GPU utilization). Such hardware-agnostic routing leads to suboptimal decisions, resulting in SLO violations, queue buildup, and underutilized GPUs. To address these challenges, we present HW-Router, a dynamic routing framework that integrates real-time hardware signals into model selection to enable accurate latency prediction and intelligent, SLO-aware routing decisions. Our approach incorporates model-specific features (architecture, size, input length) alongside hardware metrics including queue lengths, KV-cache utilization, and recent TTFT/TPOT performance, and uses a lightweight latency predictor to estimate per-model-per-GPU serving time. Evaluations across diverse workloads show that HW-Router achieves 3.4-3.9x lower end-to-end latency, 46-48 percentage points higher SLO attainment, 6-8x lower GPU load skew, and a 3.1-3.4x reduction in waiting-queue fraction compared to state-of-the-art router baselines, CARROT and IRT, with only ~200 us of additional routing overhead and no loss in output quality. These results highlight the importance of real-time hardware feedback for scalable, predictable, and well-balanced multi-LLM serving. Code is available at https://github.com/UCF-ML-Research/HW-Router.

</details>


### [77] [CoMeT-Net: Consensus Memory Template Network for Real-time Traffic Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14576)
*Tingcong Jiang,Adhwaa Alchaab,Ayman Younis,Dario Pompili*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: CoMeT-Net：面向O-RAN的轻量级共识记忆模板网络，以O(N·C)复杂度实现99.35% F1分数、10倍更低误报率和毫秒级推理，支持实时威胁隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN实时异常检测需要在资源受限的边缘部署环境中实现高准确率、低误报率和计算效率。传统方法存在计算开销大、跨域性能不一致、特征表示次优等问题，难以检测O-RAN开放接口上的微妙攻击。

Method: 提出CoMeT-Net（共识记忆模板网络）框架，包含三个核心创新：（1）结构化记忆库实现基于模板的共识投票，复杂度为O(N·C)；（2）自适应门控机制作为学习到的噪声滤波器，降低模糊特征权重；（3）对比对齐统一特征学习与分类。通过边缘服务器和Near-RT RIC xApp部署于O-RAN基础设施，支持通过PRB节流和RRC连接释放进行动态威胁缓解。

Result: 在网络流量数据集上，CoMeT-Net达到99.35%的F1分数，误报率比基线方法低10倍，在从服务器到树莓派4的各层级硬件上保持0.3-3ms推理延迟。O-RAN测试台验证表明，该方法能有效隔离攻击者，将攻击者延迟降低至1400ms以上，同时保证合法用户15-20ms的延迟。

Conclusion: CoMeT-Net通过结构化记忆模板、自适应门控和对比对齐三种创新机制，在O-RAN边缘部署场景下实现了高精度、低误报、低延迟的实时异常检测，并具备实际的威胁缓解能力，为开放无线接入网络的安全防护提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time anomaly detection in Open Radio Access Networks (O-RAN) demands high accuracy, low false alarms, and computational efficiency for resource-constrained edge deployment. Traditional methods struggle with computational overhead, inconsistent cross-domain performance, and suboptimal feature representations that miss subtle attacks on O-RAN's open interfaces. We present CoMeT-Net (Consensus Memory Template Network), a framework achieving state-of-the-art detection through three innovations: (1) structured memory banks enabling template-based consensus voting with $O(N \cdot C)$ complexity; (2) adaptive gating that downweights ambiguous features as a learned noise filter; (3) contrastive alignment unifying feature learning and classification. Deployed in O-RAN infrastructure via edge servers and Near-RT RIC xApp, CoMeT-Net enables dynamic threat mitigation through PRB throttling and RRC connection release. On network traffic datasets, CoMeT-Net achieves 99.35% F1 score with 10$\times$ lower false alarm rates than baselines while maintaining 0.3-3ms inference across hardware tiers from servers to Raspberry Pi 4. O-RAN testbed validation demonstrates effective isolation, degrading attacker latency to >1400ms while preserving 15-20ms for legitimate users.

</details>


### [78] [Enabling Telecommunication Relay Service Research with ACE Omni Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14582)
*Harrison Bourikas,Eric Kosinski,Karina Roundtree,Ronna ten Brink,Mike Woodman,Vincent Ybarra*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: MITRE公司开发了ACE Omni平台，旨在降低电信中继服务(TRS)研究的环境搭建成本，促进针对听障群体的通信服务研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TRS研究领域存在实验环境开发耗时耗力的问题，限制了实证研究的产出；需要缩小功能等效性差距并为监管提供建议。

Method: 采用设计流程、信息收集活动，开发用户画像、研究工作流程和功能需求；通过实验室试点和真实环境收集数据进行初步验证研究。

Result: 开发了ACE Omni电信研究平台，能够高效搭建TRS实验环境、模拟现有TRS技术功能并测试新技术方案；验证过程中发现并解决了挑战。

Conclusion: ACE Omni有望降低TRS研究的时间和财务成本，促进更多TRS研究，改善TRS用户的服务质量、电信体验和结果。

Abstract: The Telecommunications Relay Service (TRS) industry is comprised of organizations supported and regulated by the Federal Communications Commission that strive to provide deaf, hard of hearing, or DeafBlind individuals with functionally equivalent telecommunication services. Research that assesses current and proposed TRS technologies is used to help close functional equivalence gaps and create recommendations for regulation. TRS researchers spend a considerable amount of time and effort developing experimental environments, which has limited the field's ability to produce empirical studies. In response to this challenge, the MITRE Corporation has developed a telecommunications research platform called Accessible Communications for Everyone (ACE) Omni. This platform enables researchers to efficiently set up TRS experimental environments, emulate functionality of current TRS technologies, and test new technology solutions. Various design processes, information gathering activities, and the development of personas, research workflows, and functional requirements were leveraged in the design of ACE Omni. A preliminary validation study was conducted via in-lab piloting activities and in vivo to collect real-world TRS user data. Challenges during validation were addressed by making ACE Omni and/or study protocol modifications, and lessons learned are discussed. ACE Omni has the potential to reduce the time and financial costs associated with setting up and running TRS studies, which can promote more TRS research and enable improved service, telecommunication experiences, and outcomes for the community of TRS users.

</details>


### [79] [Evaluating the impact of adversarial traffic patterns on vanet communication using veins simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14583)
*Henry Agyapong*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过Veins仿真框架评估了多种对抗性攻击（消息泛洪、虚假信息传播、协同拥塞）对VANET性能的影响，发现对抗性流量可严重降低PDR和吞吐量，揭示了VANET的关键安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载自组网（VANETs）作为智能交通系统的关键组成部分，其开放性和动态性使其极易受到对抗性行为的影响，这些行为可能破坏通信可靠性。因此，需要深入研究对抗性流量模式对VANET性能的影响，以识别关键漏洞并为设计更具韧性的车载网络提供依据。

Method: 本文采用Veins仿真框架（集成OMNeT++和SUMO），设计并评估多种对抗性场景，包括消息泛洪攻击、虚假信息传播和协同拥塞攻击；在不同交通密度和移动性条件下进行实验，测量分组投递率（PDR）、端到端时延和网络吞吐量等关键性能指标。

Result: 实验结果表明：对抗性流量可使PDR降低高达96.55%；低密度下的消息泛洪攻击导致吞吐量下降27.89%；对抗性攻击显著降低了整体网络效率。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了VANET通信中的关键安全漏洞，强调了设计更具韧性和安全性的车载网络的必要性，并为未来构建抗攻击的VANET系统提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Vehicular Ad Hoc Networks (VANETs) are a key component of intelligent transportation systems, enabling real-time communication between vehicles. However, their open and dynamic nature makes them highly vulnerable to adversarial behaviors that can disrupt communication reliability. This paper investigates the impact of adversarial traffic patterns on VANET performance using the Veins simulation framework integrated with OMNeT++ and SUMO. We design and evaluate multiple adversarial scenarios, including message flooding, false information dissemination, and coordinated congestion attacks, under varying traffic densities and mobility conditions. The study measures key performance metrics such as packet delivery ratio (PDR), end-to-end delay, and network throughput. Experimental results show that adversarial traffic can reduce PDR by up to 96.55%, with message flooding at low density producing a throughput reduction of 27.89%, and significantly degrade overall network efficiency. The findings highlight critical vulnerabilities in VANET communication and provide insights into designing more resilient and secure vehicular networks.

</details>


### [80] [Comparing UPF Dataplane I/O Modes in a Cloud-Native 5G Core: AF_PACKET, AF_XDP, CNDP, and DPDK on SD-Core BESS-UPF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14589)
*Shiva Valia,Nitin Rajput,Aditya Gairola,Vipin Rathi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在相同开源UPF上对四种数据包I/O模式（AF_PACKET、AF_XDP、CNDP、DPDK）进行公平对比，发现DPDK凭借绕过AF_XDP的worker数量限制在高端扩展性上领先，但AF_XDP单核效率最优；CNDP和AF_XDP适合资源受限的云原生场景，同时揭示了Kubernetes配置等实际部署陷阱对性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UPF（用户面功能）承载5G网络中的所有用户面流量，其吞吐量取决于数据包在NIC与应用程序之间的传输方式，即数据包I/O模式。目前存在多种I/O模式（AF_PACKET、AF_XDP、CNDP、DPDK），但缺乏在相同开源UPF上对这些模式进行公平、系统的对比研究，特别是在云原生5G部署场景下的实际表现和部署陷阱尚不明确。

Method: 在单一开源UPF（SD-Core BESS-UPF）上，通过仅更换BESS端口驱动的方式，对四种I/O模式（AF_PACKET、AF_XDP、CNDP、DPDK）进行控制变量对比。测试环境为：以Charmed operator部署在Canonical Kubernetes上，使用Intel XXV710/i40e网卡。对每种模式分析其架构、数据路径、内存模型和部署需求，并测量吞吐量、延迟、CPU使用率和稳定性。测试遵循RFC 2544的NDR（非丢弃速率）标准，并在64字节小包场景下进行，最终通过解耦的O-RAN 5G RAN和商业UE进行端到端验证。

Result: 在XXV710/i40e测试平台上（64字节，RFC 2544 NDR）：AF_PACKET达到0.25 Mpps；CNDP在2个worker下达5.52 Mpps，AF_XDP在2个worker下达6.47 Mpps；DPDK在4个worker下达10.30 Mpps，8个worker下达13.09 Mpps，且延迟最低（平均8.1微秒）。在相同worker数量下，三种内核旁路模式性能接近，AF_XDP单核效率领先；DPDK的优势源于其原生PMD通过vfio-pci避开了AF_XDP的每netdev socket限制（该限制将CNDP和AF_XDP固定在2个worker），而非每包处理效率更高。此外，记录了合成基准中未出现的部署陷阱，如Kubernetes limits.cpu配置错误会静默地将DPDK吞吐量减半。

Conclusion: CNDP和AF_XDP在无法承担大页内存、vfio-pci和核心隔离成本的场景下是云原生的最佳选择；DPDK则在专用主机上具有合理性。本文为运营商在云原生5G部署中选择UPF数据平面模式提供了并列参考，强调了实际部署环境与合成基准测试之间的差异。

Abstract: The User Plane Function (UPF) carries all user-plane traffic in a 5G network, and its throughput depends on how packets move between the NIC and the application, that is, on the packet I/O mode. We compare four widely used modes, AF_PACKET, AF_XDP, the Cloud Native Data Plane (CNDP), and the Data Plane Development Kit (DPDK), on a single open-source UPF. Using SD-Core BESS-UPF deployed as a Charmed operator on Intel XXV710 NICs in Canonical Kubernetes, we run the same GTP-U/PDR/FAR/QER pipeline under each mode and change only the BESS port driver, so any difference is attributable to the I/O backend. For each mode we describe its architecture, datapath, memory model, and deployment requirements, and we measure throughput, latency, CPU usage, and stability; the deployment is also validated end-to-end against a disaggregated O-RAN 5G RAN with a commercial UE.
  On an XXV710/i40e testbed (NDR per RFC 2544) at 64 B, AF_PACKET reaches 0.25 Mpps, CNDP 5.52 and AF_XDP 6.47 Mpps at 2 workers, and DPDK 10.30 Mpps at 4 workers and 13.09 at 8, with the lowest latency (8.1 microseconds average). At matched worker counts the three kernel-bypass modes are within noise and AF_XDP leads per core; DPDK's advantage is a scaling-ceiling effect, since its native PMD over vfio-pci escapes the per-netdev AF_XDP socket limit that pins CNDP and AF_XDP at two workers, not a per-packet efficiency win. CNDP and AF_XDP are the cloud-native sweet spot when hugepages, vfio-pci, and isolated cores are unaffordable; DPDK is justified on dedicated hosts. We also document deployment pitfalls absent from synthetic benchmarks, including a Kubernetes limits.cpu mis-setting that silently halved DPDK throughput. The paper is a side-by-side reference for operators choosing a UPF dataplane mode in a cloud-native 5G deployment.

</details>


### [81] [Low-Latency Spatial-Provenance Recovery Methods for Privacy-Constrained Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14592)
*Manish Bansal,J. Harshan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对多跳V2X网络中低延迟位置数据采集的隐私保护需求，提出了BSE和TSE两种空间溯源协议，通过跳过机制在车辆间权衡隐私特性，实现了比现有方案更低的端到端延迟和更高的溯源可靠性，在5G环境下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多跳V2X网络中，RSU需要以低延迟方式收集车辆位置信息以支持实时基于位置的服务，同时需保护车辆隐私。现有联合数据-空间溯源技术虽然能在一定程度上保护车辆隐私，但在端到端延迟有严格时限要求时无法满足需求。因此，需要设计满足严格延迟约束且能管理隐私问题的空间溯源方法。

Method: 提出了两种新颖协议：双段嵌入（BSE）和三段嵌入（TSE）。这两种协议为联合数据-空间溯源提供了跳过机制，在车辆之间权衡隐私特性。通过广泛的理论框架对提出的方案在可靠性、隐私性和通信开销方面进行分析。

Result: 与基线方案相比，所提出的协议具有更低的端到端延迟、更高的溯源重建可靠性，并且相对于RSU保持相同水平的隐私性。使用实际无线电参数验证了延迟增益，研究表明在5G协议栈上实现时，所提出的协议在延迟方面具有显著优势。

Conclusion: BSE和TSE协议通过跳过机制有效平衡了V2X网络中的延迟与隐私需求，在保持隐私水平的同时显著降低了端到端延迟，特别适用于5G环境下的实时位置服务场景。

Abstract: In multihop Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) networks, Road Side Units (RSUs) intend to collect information on vehicles' location in a low-latency manner while respecting their privacy constraints to support real-time location-based services. To facilitate data collection, provenance is known to ensure trust and accountability of data. Although existing joint data- and spatial-provenance techniques preserve the privacy of vehicles up to a certain granularity with respect to the RSU and other vehicles, they are unsuitable when stringent deadlines are imposed on the end-to-end delay on the packets. As a consequence, there is a need for designing spatial-provenance methods for V2X networks that satisfy stringent deadlines on the end-to-end delays while managing the privacy concerns. To fill this research gap, we propose two novel protocols, namely: Bi-Segment Embedding (BSE) and Tri-Segment Embedding (TSE), which provide a skipping mechanism for joint data- and spatial-provenance while trading off privacy features among the vehicles. Through an extensive theoretical framework, we provide an analysis of the proposed schemes in terms of reliability, privacy, and communication overhead. When compared to the baselines, our protocols offer lower end-to-end delay, higher reliability in provenance reconstruction, and the same level of privacy with respect to the RSU. We validate latency gains using practical radio parameters, and our study reveals that our proposed protocols offer significant benefits in latency when implemented over a 5G stack.

</details>


### [82] [BC-DIR: Bandit-Controlled Deadline-Aware Incremental Redundancy for QUIC in V2X Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14593)
*Weihao Liu,He Sun,Chen-Khong Tham*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出BC-DIR框架，通过无速率编码结合上下文赌博机在线自适应冗余配置，在QUIC-based V2X传输中实现截止时间感知的增量冗余修复，在拥堵场景下提升10%-40%完成率，良好网络条件下降低约1%开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V2X通信需要在高度动态无线条件下实现及时可靠的消息传输。现有将前向纠错(FEC)集成到QUIC的方案主要依赖主动冗余，一旦丢包超过配置码字的纠错能力就回退到重传机制，导致在突发丢包场景下恢复效率低下，在网络条件良好时产生不必要的开销。

Method: 本文提出了一种基于QUIC的V2X传输框架——Bandit-Controlled Deadline-Aware Incremental Redundancy (BC-DIR)。该框架结合无速率编码、软解码截止时间和冗余余量机制，允许在可用传输预算内触发修复，并注入超出即时缺额的额外修复符号以改善突发丢包恢复；同时采用上下文赌博机控制器根据端到端反馈在线自适应调整冗余配置。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真验证了分析结果。BC-DIR在基于QUIC的传输栈中实现，并在Veins/OMNeT++中针对拥堵城市V2X场景和稳定网络条件进行评估。实验表明：在不同交通拥塞水平下，BC-DIR在拥堵V2X场景中将完成率比基准方案提高10%-40%；在网络条件良好时，相比原生QUIC甚至能减少约1%的开销。

Conclusion: BC-DIR通过无速率编码与在线自适应冗余配置的协同设计，有效解决了V2X通信中突发丢包修复与传输开销之间的权衡问题，在保障截止时间约束可靠性的同时实现了场景自适应的传输优化。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communications require timely and reliable message delivery under highly dynamic wireless conditions. Existing approaches that integrate forward error correction (FEC) into QUIC rely mainly on proactive redundancy and fall back to retransmission once losses exceed the correction capability of the configured code, leading to inefficient recovery under burst loss and unnecessary overhead when network conditions are favorable. This paper presents a Bandit-Controlled Deadline-Aware Incremental Redundancy (BC-DIR) framework for QUIC-based V2X transport. BC-DIR combines rateless coding with a soft decoding deadline and a redundancy margin, enabling repair to be triggered within the available delivery budget while injecting additional repair symbols beyond the immediate deficit to improve recovery under burst loss. A contextual bandit controller further adapts the redundancy configuration online according to end-to-end feedback. We also develop a deadline-constrained reliability analysis under burst loss, showing the advantage of the proposed repair mechanism over conventional retransmission and the existence of an optimal redundancy margin. Monte Carlo simulations validate the analytical results. BC-DIR is implemented in a QUIC-based transport stack and evaluated in Veins/OMNeT++ under both congested urban V2X scenarios and stable network conditions. Experimental results show that, across different traffic congestion levels, BC-DIR improves completion ratio by 10\%--40\% over benchmark schemes in congested V2X scenarios, while under favorable network conditions it can even reduce overhead by about 1\% compared with native QUIC.

</details>


### [83] [Cyberspace Search Intentions as Leading Indicators for Proactive Traffic Hotspot Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14595)
*Hangli Ge,Dizhi Huang,Takeshi Kawasaki,Noboru Koshizuka*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种网络-物理数据驱动框架，利用用户在线路线搜索记录作为早期指标，主动检测高速公路交通热点和热区。通过构建ODT张量模型处理大规模搜索数据，并结合实际交通量验证，证明了在线搜索行为可作为预测交通动态的有效代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交通监测方法通常依赖物理传感器数据，存在滞后性，难以实现交通热点和热区的主动预警。研究者发现用户在 cyberspace 中的在线搜索行为（如路线查询）可能早于实际出行发生，因此希望利用这些网络数据作为交通需求的早期指示器，从而在拥堵形成前进行预测和干预。

Method: 1. 构建网络-物理数据驱动框架，将用户在线搜索记录与实际交通系统关联；2. 提出Origin Destination Time (ODT) 张量模型，用于表征路线搜索数据的时空结构并加速计算；3. 基于目的地流入序列，开发系统化方法自动识别异常激增模式，以检测新兴交通热点并进一步识别热区；4. 使用覆盖2,728个互通立交节点的一年期真实数据集进行实验，并将搜索数据与实际交通量进行整合对比。

Result: 1. 搜索强度与实际交通流量之间存在强相关性，验证了在线搜索行为作为交通动态预测代理的可靠性；2. ODT张量模型能够有效处理大规模、不规则的搜索记录数据；3. 所提框架可基于网络搜索数据主动监测交通热点和热区；4. 路线搜索记录在 cyberspace 中可被有效用于交通主动监测，展现了拥堵模式早期预测的潜力。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了将 cyberspace 中的用户路线搜索数据与物理交通系统相结合的可行性与有效性。ODT张量模型为大规模时空搜索数据的高效处理提供了新思路，所提出的框架能够实现交通热点和热区的主动检测，为智能交通系统中的早期拥堵预测和交通管理决策提供了新的数据驱动方法。

Abstract: This study proposes a cyber-physical data-driven framework for proactive detection of highway traffic hotspots and hot regions. The proposed framework bridges users' online search records in cyberspace as early indicator. To handle large-scale and irregular search records, we propose an Origin Destination Time (ODT) tensor model to represent the spatio-temporal structure of route search data and accelerate computation. Using destination-wise inflow sequences derived from these records, we develop a systematic method to automatically identify anomalous surges that indicate emerging traffic hotspots and further the regions. To validate the framework, we conduct experiments using a one-year real-world dataset covering 2,728 interchange (IC) nodes within a highway network. Furthermore, we integrate and compare search data with actual traffic volumes for evaluation. The results reveal a strong correlation between search intensity and traffic flow, demonstrating that online search behavior serves as a reliable proxy for anticipating traffic dynamics. These findings suggest that route search records in cyberspace can be effectively utilized for proactive traffic monitoring and highlight the potential for early prediction of congestion patterns.

</details>


### [84] [Real-Time Patient Monitoring with Heterogeneous Systems Using DDS-Based Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14597)
*Muhammad Dikko Gambo,MD Sakibul Islam,Basem Almadani,Farouq Aliyu,Abdullahi Sani Shuaibu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于DDS中间件的实时患者监护系统，通过分层架构和QoS策略配置，实验证明其在可靠性上优于传统socket方案，适用于临床场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时患者监护需要低延迟、高可靠且能跨异构医院部署扩展的通信系统，现有方案难以同时满足这些需求。

Method: 提出基于DDS中间件的监护系统，采用模块化DDS域参与者架构部署于病房与病区级应用，通过分层数据总线连接；配置并评估Reliable和Best Effort两种QoS策略；开发原型系统模拟临床监护工作流，并与基于socket的消息传输进行实验对比。

Result: DDS配合Reliable QoS在测试场景中避免了丢包，在网络负载下比socket提供更可靠的传输；验证了DDS作为实时临床通信中间件的可行性。

Conclusion: DDS是一种适用于对数据一致性交付有要求的实时临床通信场景的实用中间件方案，其QoS策略可有效平衡传输保证与通信开销。

Abstract: Real-time patient monitoring requires communication systems that maintain low latency and high reliability while scaling across heterogeneous hospital deployments. This paper presents a middleware-based monitoring system that uses the Data Distribution Service (DDS) to coordinate data exchange among distributed medical components. The system architecture consists of modular DDS domain participants deployed across patient rooms and ward-level applications, connected through a layered data bus structure. Quality of Service (QoS) policies, including Reliable and Best Effort, are configured and evaluated to examine trade-offs between delivery guarantees and communication overhead. A prototype implementation is developed to emulate clinical monitoring workflows, and experiments are conducted in comparison with socket-based messaging. The evaluation indicates that DDS with Reliable QoS avoids packet loss in the tested scenarios and provides more dependable delivery than sockets under network load. These results support the use of DDS as a practical middleware option for real-time clinical communication where consistent data delivery is required.

</details>


### [85] [Intelligent Base Station Deployment in Urban Wireless Networks: A Geographic Data-Informed Digital Twin Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14599)
*Zhenyu Tao,Yuxuan Li,Wei Xu,Yongming Huang,Xiaohu You*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合地理数据驱动数字孪生与深度强化学习的智能基站部署框架，无需现场测量即可实现高精度、低开销的大规模宏基站部署优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模基站（BS）部署优化具有挑战性，因为该问题依赖于特定站点的无线电传播和用户空间分布，而这些信息在部署前难以获取。现有方法通常需要现场测量、真实用户轨迹或耗尽的射线追踪，成本高昂且难以扩展。

Method: 提出了一种智能基站部署框架，将地理数据驱动的无线网络数字孪生（DT）与深度强化学习（DRL）相结合。该DT包含：（1）采用混合输入表示的无样本无线地图预测模型，实现公里级信号强度毫秒级估计；（2）基于扩散的生成模型用于轨迹合成，共同表征信道和用户分布。在此基础上，将基站部署建模为多步马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），通过空间结构化DRL算法求解，并引入局部搜索过程和基于Wasserstein距离的部署缓存以高效探索大规模组合解空间。

Result: 在真实城市场景中的实验表明：（1）地理数据驱动的DT达到了与100样本预测相当的精度；（2）智能基站部署框架达到了理想基准性能的98.9%；（3）优化开销降低超过99%。

Conclusion: 该研究成功实现了仅利用开放地理数据、无需现场测量或真实用户轨迹的宏基站部署优化。所提出的地理数据驱动数字孪生与深度强化学习相结合的框架，在保持高精度的同时显著降低了计算成本，为大规模城市无线网络部署提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: The placement of base station (BS) is a fundamental determinant of coverage and capacity of urban wireless networks. Yet large-scale BS deployment optimization remains challenging due to its dependency on site-specific radio propagation and user spatial distributions, both of which are unfortunately difficult to obtain prior to deployment. To overcome this barrier, we propose an intelligent BS deployment framework that integrates a geographic data-informed wireless network digital twin (DT) with deep reinforcement learning (DRL), enabling sample-free macro BS deployment optimization from solely open geographic data, without on-site measurements, real user trajectories, or exhaustive ray tracing. The proposed DT incorporates a sample-free radio map prediction model with hybrid input representation to achieve kilometer-scale signal strength estimation in milliseconds, complemented by a diffusion-based generative model for trajectory synthesis to collectively characterize channel and user distributions. Leveraging the DT as a virtual training environment, we formulate BS deployment as a multi-step Markov decision process (MDP) and solve it via a spatially structured DRL algorithm. A local search process and a Wasserstein distance-based deployment buffer are further incorporated to efficiently explore the large combinatorial solution space. Experimental results in real-world urban scenarios demonstrate that the geographic data-informed DT attains accuracy comparable to 100-sample-based prediction, and the intelligent BS deployment framework achieves up to 98.9% of the idealized benchmark performance while reducing optimization overhead by over 99%.

</details>


### [86] [Demo: Real-time Generative Multicasting with On-Device Intent-aware Semantic Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14600)
*Xinkai Liu,Mahdi Boloursaz Mashhadi,Yi Ma,Rahim Tafazolli*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于端侧意图感知语义分解的生成式多播方案，通过广播语义图并仅传输用户意图相关的子信号类别，利用端侧生成模型合成非意图内容，显著降低多播资源消耗，并在Edge TPU上实现实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有/新兴的多媒体多播应用需要为不同用户传输完整视频内容，导致无线/网络资源消耗巨大；同时，不同用户往往只关注视频中的特定语义内容（即接收意图不同），存在严重的资源浪费。因此，需要一种能够根据多用户接收意图进行语义分解、并显著降低无线/网络资源需求的新型多播方案。

Method: 提出生成式多播（Generative Multicasting）框架，核心方法包括：(1) 基于DNN的语义分割：在发送端从源视频中提取语义图（semantic map）；(2) 意图感知的语义分解：根据多用户接收意图将视频分解为多个子信号类别（sub-signal classes）；(3) 分层广播机制：通过共享无线/网络资源向所有用户广播语义图，仅使用正交资源传输各用户意图对应的子信号类别；(4) 端侧生成式重建：用户端结合接收到的意图类别与从语义图本地生成的非意图类别，进行部分重建、部分合成；(5) 自适应速率控制：推导重建/合成的率-失真/感知曲线，自适应设置语义图和意图类别的压缩速率。系统在Google Coral Edge TPU（4 TOPS, int8）上实现实时运行。

Result: 生成式多播显著减少了现有/新兴多媒体多播应用所需的无线/网络资源；系统在Google Coral Edge TPU上实现了实时性能；首次实现了生成式多播的演示，代表了端侧生成式语义通信（on-device generative SemCom）的重大进展。

Conclusion: 本文提出的生成式多播通过意图感知的语义分解与端侧生成模型协同，实现了高效的资源利用，为语义通信在多播场景中的实际部署提供了可行方案，并推动了端侧生成式语义通信的发展。

Abstract: We present a demonstration for generative multicasting with on-device, intent-aware semantic decomposition. At the transmitter, DNN-based segmentation extracts a semantic map from the source video, decomposing it into multiple sub-signal classes based on multi-user receiver intents. The transmitter broadcasts the semantic map to all users over shared wireless/network resources, thereby utilizing orthogonal resources only to transmit the sub-signal classes intended for each user. Users partially reconstruct and partially synthesize the signal by combining the received intended classes with non-intended classes locally synthesized by a generative model from the semantic map. We derive the rate-distortion/perception curves for reconstruction/synthesis with the generative model, to adaptively set compression rates for the semantic map and intended classes. Generative multicasting significantly reduces the wireless/network resources required for existing/emerging multimedia multicasting applications. The system is real-time on a Google Coral Edge TPU with 4 TOPS (int8). This is the first demonstration of generative multicasting representing a substantial advancement in on-device generative SemCom.

</details>


### [87] [OneBarrier: What a Network Must Provide for Transparent Fault Tolerance to Be Free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14601)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: OneBarrier通过in-network total-order fabric（1Pipe）和低成本确定性shim，消除了传统透明容错的三大性能代价，使15个未修改应用（Redis、Nginx、PostgreSQL等）实现微秒级开销的崩溃恢复，证明在满足特定网络条件下容错可以是"属性而非代价"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明容错技术（使未经修改的服务器二进制程序在崩溃后存活）已追求四十年但未投入生产。现有方案在关键路径上付出三个代价：记录消息到达顺序以支持重放、协调一致性快照、以及每个回复必须等待产生它的状态持久化。作者认为这些代价并非固有，而是网络既不保证顺序也不保证交付的结果。

Method: OneBarrier系统，基于in-network total-order fabric（1Pipe）实现微秒级往返延迟。确定性保障通过用户空间shim实现：虚拟时间、虚拟化随机性、以及share-nothing分片替代线程调度。核心协议使用TLA+进行机器验证。

Result: 用户空间shim以2-10%的开销为未修改二进制程序实现确定性。15个未修改应用程序实现字节级精确恢复，崩溃注入验证线性化exactly-once历史。核心协议通过TLA+机器验证。Barrier内持久化写仅增加4.6μs，barrier后则增加3ms。

Conclusion: 在满足了四个条件（Order、Barrier、Durability、Determinism）的网络环境下，透明容错可以成为一种"属性"而非"代价"。OneBarrier系统实现了这一目标：15个未经修改的应用程序（包括Redis、Memcached、Nginx、Node.js和多进程PostgreSQL）能够实现字节级精确的恢复；崩溃注入实验确认了线性化、exactly-once的执行历史；核心协议已通过TLA+机器验证。关键性能数据：barrier内的持久化写操作仅增加4.6微秒延迟，而barrier后的相同操作则增加3毫秒。

Abstract: Transparent fault tolerance -- making an unmodified server binary survive crashes -- has been pursued for four decades without reaching production. Every attempt paid three costs on the critical path: recording message arrival order for replay, coordinating a consistent snapshot, and holding each reply until the state that produced it was durable. This paper argues the costs are not intrinsic: they are the price of a network that guarantees neither order nor delivery. We state four conditions under which all three vanish. Three concern the network: Order (messages are delivered in one global sequence), Barrier (delivery is confirmed by a commit barrier), and Durability (each message is replicated to backups before its barrier completes). The fourth, Determinism, falls to the host: a user-space shim closes it for unmodified binaries at 2-10% overhead -- virtual time, virtualized randomness, and share-nothing sharding in place of thread scheduling. OneBarrier realizes all four conditions over an in-network total-order fabric (1Pipe) with microsecond round trips. Fifteen unmodified applications -- including Redis, Memcached, Nginx, Node.js, and a multi-process PostgreSQL -- recover byte-identically, and crash injection confirms linearizable, exactly-once histories; the core protocols are machine-checked in TLA+. A durable write placed inside the barrier adds 4.6 microseconds to a request; the same write placed after it adds three milliseconds. On a network that meets the conditions, fault tolerance is a property, not a tax.

</details>


### [88] [Extend the Safety Horizon for Intelligent Transportation Systems through Semantic-Aware Cooperative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14603)
*Chun-Yeow Yeoh,Chee Keong Tan,Joanne Mun-Yee Lim,Heng-Siong Lim*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出HMS-SCP框架，通过多尺度语义感知和联合信源信道编码实现带宽高效的协同感知，在极端压缩和信道衰落条件下仍保持实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同感知通过V2X通信扩展感知范围，但密集城市环境中累积带宽需求可能超出网络容量，危及安全关键型C-ITS功能。现有中间融合方法虽能平衡带宽与精度，但在高密度场景下仍面临网络拥塞挑战。

Method: 提出HMS-SCP（分层多尺度语义感知协同感知）框架：1) 空间重要性预测器识别各尺度任务相关网格元素；2) 直接映射为复数值符号进行联合信源信道编码（JSCC）；3) 利用多尺度结构语义冗余增强抗噪声能力，无需高维符号投影；4) 实现超低符号率传输。

Result: 在OPV2V（仿真）和DAIR-V2X（真实世界）数据集上的评估表明：HMS-SCP在严重瑞利衰落和极端压缩比下有效防止性能崩溃，保持高置信度远场检测，实时延迟低于16ms，满足动态V2X环境安全关键阈值要求。

Conclusion: HMS-SCP通过多尺度语义冗余和JSCC设计，实现了鲁棒、带宽高效的面向任务的语义通信，为高密度车载环境下的协同感知提供了可行的实用部署方案。

Abstract: Cooperative perception enables vehicles and infrastructure to exchange sensor data via Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communication, extending sensing coverage beyond occlusions and mitigating blind spots. While critical for autonomous driving and safety, practical deployments often rely on bandwidth-efficient late fusion. Recently, intermediate fusion has emerged as a promising approach for an optimal bandwidth-accuracy trade-off. However, in dense urban environments, cumulative bandwidth demands can overwhelm network capacity, potentially compromising safety-critical Cooperative Intelligent Transport Systems (C-ITS) functions. To alleviate these problems, this paper proposes Hierarchical Multi-Scale Semantic-Aware Cooperative Perception (HMS-SCP), a robust noise-resilient and bandwidth-efficient framework for task-oriented semantic communication in cooperative perception. HMS-SCP employs a spatial importance predictor to identify task-relevant grid elements at each scale, which are then directly mapped into complex-valued symbols for Joint Source-Channel Coding (JSCC). Unlike prior methods that rely on high-dimensional symbol projections for robustness, HMS-SCP exploits structural semantic redundancy across multiple scales to enhance resilience against channel noise, while maintaining an ultra-low symbol rate. This design significantly reduces bandwidth consumption and mitigates network congestion in high-density vehicular environments. Extensive evaluations on the simulated OPV2V and real-world DAIR-V2X datasets demonstrate that HMS-SCP effectively prevents performance collapse under severe Rayleigh fading and extreme compression ratio, maintaining high-confidence far-field detection with a real-time latency of below 16~ms, well within the safety-critical thresholds for dynamic V2X environments.

</details>


### [89] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for 6G AI-RAN: A Comprehensive Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14877)
*Jie Lu,Peihao Yan,Qijun Wang,Ruxin Lin,Huacheng Zeng*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 首篇DRL赋能6G Open AI-RAN的系统性综述，构建了O-RAN感知的DRL问题建模框架，全面覆盖从基础理论到前沿方向的研究进展，并指明了样本高效、安全、可扩展、可部署的智能控制发展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络演进要求RAN成为可编程、智能的控制平台，需持续适应异构业务、动态环境和竞争性能目标。O-RAN提供了开放接口、解耦架构和多时间尺度控制环路来支撑这一转型，DRL为不确定性下的序贯决策优化提供了自然框架。然而，现有综述要么泛泛讨论O-RAN中的AI/ML，要么仅关注孤立的DRL用例，缺乏DRL方法论、O-RAN架构和运营部署之间的系统性关联。

Method: 本文首次对Open AI-RAN中的DRL进行专门且全面的综述。研究方法包括：(1) 回顾无模型、基于模型、离线、安全、多智能体、联邦和迁移学习等DRL基础理论；(2) 构建O-RAN感知框架，通过状态、观测、动作、奖励、约束和时间结构来形式化RAN控制问题；(3) 对DRL应用进行分类，涵盖无线资源管理、移动性管理、干扰控制、流量引导、能效、网络切片、通感一体化、安全和大规模MIMO等领域；(4) 考察多智能体和联邦协调、基础模型与智能体AI、可信DRL、仿真到现实迁移、持续适应、资源高效推理和强化学习运营等前沿方向；(5) 综述实验平台、基准测试、标准和产业活动。

Result: 建立了DRL与O-RAN架构之间的系统性连接框架，全面梳理了DRL在O-RAN各功能领域的应用现状，识别了样本高效、安全、可扩展、可互操作和可部署的DRL控制研究方向，为6G Open AI-RAN的发展提供了理论指导和实践参考。

Conclusion: DRL是实现6G Open AI-RAN智能控制的关键使能技术。未来研究应聚焦于样本效率提升、安全性保障、规模可扩展性、系统互操作性和实际可部署性，推动DRL从理论研究走向工程实践，助力6G网络的智能化演进。

Abstract: The evolution toward sixth-generation (6G) networks is transforming the radio access network (RAN) into a programmable and intelligent control platform that must continuously adapt to heterogeneous services, dynamic environments, and competing performance objectives. Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) provides the open interfaces, disaggregated architecture, and multi-timescale control loops needed to support this transformation, while deep reinforcement learning (DRL) offers a natural framework for optimizing sequential decisions under uncertainty. However, existing surveys either address artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) in O-RAN broadly or focus on isolated DRL use cases, leaving a gap in the systematic connection between DRL methodology, O-RAN architecture, and operational deployment. To the best of our knowledge, this article presents the first dedicated and comprehensive survey of DRL for Open AI-RAN. We review the foundations of model-free, model-based, offline, safe, multi-agent, federated, and transfer learning, and provide an O-RAN-aware framework for formulating RAN control problems through states, observations, actions, rewards, constraints, and temporal structure. We classify DRL applications across radio resource management, mobility management, interference control, traffic steering, energy efficiency, network slicing, integrated sensing and communication, security, and massive MIMO. We further examine multi-agent and federated coordination, foundation models and agentic AI, trustworthy DRL, sim-to-real transfer, continual adaptation, resource-efficient inference, and reinforcement learning operations. Finally, we review experimental platforms, benchmarks, standards, and industry activities, and identify research directions toward sample-efficient, safe, scalable, interoperable, and deployable DRL control for 6G Open AI-RAN.

</details>


### [90] [Agentic AI-Enabled Solar-Powered High-Altitude Platforms for Sustainable SAGINs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15087)
*Haoxiang Luo,Bang Huang,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向太阳能高空平台的智能体AI框架，通过多时间尺度闭环控制实现通信、计算与能量的联合优化，提升了空天地一体化网络在灾害场景下的能效与任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空天地一体化网络（SAGINs）虽能扩展连接，但其通信、计算和平台操作产生了紧密耦合的能耗需求。太阳能驱动的高空平台（HAPs）通过提供持久区域覆盖、可再生能源收集和机载计算，构成了有前景的中间层。然而，实现这一潜力需要超越单个链路或处理器的优化，因为无线电传输、任务执行、回程链路使用和电池保护共享一个共同的能量预算。

Method: 本文提出了一种HAP原生智能体AI框架。该框架持续感知通信、计算、能量、移动性和任务状态；调用定量工具进行预测和验证；并通过闭环控制回路协调可执行动作。采用多时间尺度设计，将快速无线电控制与任务编排及长期能量规划分离。

Result: 通过灾难恢复案例研究展示了该框架如何应对回程拥塞、流量激增和太阳能发电下降等问题，在能量效率、任务完成率和延迟方面优于其他基线方法。

Conclusion: 可信控制、协作多HAP编排和数字孪生辅助的终身自适应是实现可部署、可持续和有弹性的SAGIN智能的关键步骤。

Abstract: Space-Air-Ground Integrated Networks (SAGINs) can extend connectivity, but their communication, computing, and platform operations create tightly coupled energy demands. Solar-powered High-Altitude Platforms (HAPs) offer a promising middle layer by combining persistent regional coverage, renewable-energy harvesting, and onboard computing. However, realizing this potential requires more than optimizing individual links or processors, as radio transmission, task execution, backhaul use, and battery preservation share a common energy budget. Therefore, we introduce a HAP-native Agentic AI framework. It continuously perceives communication, computing, energy, mobility, and mission states; invokes quantitative tools for prediction and verification; and coordinates executable actions through a closed control loop. Then, a multi-timescale design separates fast radio control from task orchestration and long-term energy planning. Furthermore, a disaster-recovery case study illustrates how the framework responds to backhaul congestion, traffic surges, and declining solar generation, improving energy efficiency, task completion, and latency over other baselines. We finally identify trustworthy control, collaborative multi-HAP orchestration, and digital-twin-assisted lifelong adaptation as key steps toward deployable, sustainable, and resilient SAGIN intelligence.

</details>


### [91] [ISAC in 3GPP: Evolution Toward 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15283)
*Neeraj Varshney*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文综述3GPP从R19到R20的ISAC标准化演进，涵盖需求、信道模型、物理层/协议/架构设计，并指出波形设计、多节点协调、隐私等关键挑战，为6G ISAC标准化提供系统性解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着6G发展，集成感知与通信（ISAC）成为3GPP演进的重要方向，蜂窝网络除提供连接性外还需具备环境感知能力。本文旨在梳理3GPP从Release 19可行性研究到Release 20无线、协议及架构研究的标准化轨迹，为6G ISAC的实际部署提供标准视角的解读。

Method: 本文采用综述分析方法，系统梳理3GPP标准化进程中的关键要素：包括业务需求分析、感知拓扑结构、超越TR 38.901的信道模型演进、RAN1物理层设计、RAN2/RAN3系统层面影响，以及感知辅助通信的作用；同时综合评估波形与参考信号设计、多节点协调、感知数据上报、业务开放、隐私保护和实现约束等未解决问题。

Result: 论文建立了从业务层动机到物理层、协议层和架构层影响的完整映射，明确了3GPP向实用化6G ISAC支持演进的标准路径；识别了Release 19至Release 20阶段已确立的需求、正在进行的研究假设以及可能的前进方向，为后续标准化工作提供了系统性参考框架。

Conclusion: 该综述通过标准中心的视角，连接了服务级需求与底层技术实现，揭示了3GPP ISAC标准化进程中的关键进展与挑战。未解决的问题主要集中在波形与参考信号联合设计、多节点协同机制、感知数据的高效上报、网络能力开放、用户隐私保护及工程实现约束等方面，这些将是Release 20及后续版本需要重点突破的方向。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) is emerging as an important direction in the Third Generation Partnership Project (3GPP) evolution toward 6G because it allows cellular networks to provide environmental awareness in addition to connectivity. This paper surveys the current 3GPP trajectory from Release~19 feasibility studies to Release~20 radio, protocol, and architecture studies, while distinguishing established requirements, ongoing study assumptions, and possible forward directions. The survey covers service requirements, sensing topologies, channel model evolution beyond 3GPP Technical Report (TR)~38.901, Radio Access Network Working Group~1 (RAN1) physical layer design, Radio Access Network Working Groups~2 and~3 (RAN2 and RAN3) system implications, and the role of sensing-assisted communication. It also synthesizes the main unresolved issues in waveform and reference signal design, multi-node coordination, sensing data reporting, service exposure, privacy, and implementation constraints. By connecting service-level motivations to physical layer, protocol, and architecture implications, the paper provides a standards-centric reading of how 3GPP may evolve toward practical 6G ISAC support.

</details>


### [92] [Exploring the Suitability of QUIC for the Internet of Things](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15366)
*Carles Gomez,Nika Soltani-Tehrani,Jon Crowcroft*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了QUIC协议在物联网(IoT)场景中的适用性，提出了一种针对资源受限环境优化的QUIC配置方案，并正在进行IETF标准化推进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QUIC协议最初为提升Web流量性能而设计，主要针对TCP/TLS协议栈的问题进行优化，并未考虑物联网场景的资源约束特性。然而，随着QUIC的普及度提升和物联网的持续扩展，研究QUIC在IoT环境中的适用性变得必要且有价值。

Method: 利用QUIC的可选功能特性，提出、讨论并评估一种专门针对物联网场景的QUIC配置方案（profile）。

Result: 提出了一种适用于物联网环境的QUIC配置方案，该方案正在进行IETF标准化审议。

Conclusion: QUIC协议通过合理的功能裁剪和优化配置，可以在资源受限的物联网环境中提供可行的连接方案，为IoT设备带来新的连接机会。

Abstract: QUIC is an emerging transport-layer protocol that provides reliability and security. QUIC was designed to overcome issues from other protocol stacks used in the Internet, such as TCP/TLS, especially focusing on web traffic performance improvement. Therefore, QUIC was not conceived for Internet of Things (IoT) scenarios, which are characterized by significant resource constraints. However, as QUIC prominance increases, and the IoT continues to expand, QUIC may offer connectivity opportunities for IoT devices. In this paper, we explore the suitability of QUIC for IoT environments. Leveraging optional functionality, we propose, discuss, and evaluate a QUIC profile for IoT scenarios that is currently being considered for IETF standardization.

</details>


### [93] [Adaptive Bridge: A Proxy-Based Decoupling Layer for Mitigating DDS Backpressure in ROS 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15380)
*Kaushalraj Puwar*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Adaptive Bridge是一种ROS 2代理层，通过将RELIABLE主题拆分为关键/非关键双通道并实施自适应速率限制，彻底隔离DDS背压影响，使关键节点延迟从15秒降至2ms以内，同时保证发布者吞吐量恒定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于DDS的ROS 2系统中，RELIABLE主题上的单个慢速订阅者会产生背压（backpressure），导致共享同一发布者的所有订阅者（包括安全关键的本地节点）的吞吐量和延迟性能下降。现有机制缺乏有效的隔离手段，使得关键任务节点易受非关键节点行为的影响。

Method: 提出Adaptive Bridge，一种基于代理的解耦层，通过主题分割和自适应速率控制实现关键订阅者与非关键订阅者的隔离。具体机制包括：(1) 代理订阅原始主题并通过两个独立的DDS写入器重新发布——RELIABLE模式用于关键消费者，BEST_EFFORT模式用于非关键消费者，从而切断背压传播的因果链；(2) 基于探测的分类器（probe-based classifier）采用滞后机制（hysteresis）监控订阅者健康状态，并实时调整非关键流的速率限制。

Result: 在Gilbert-Elliot突发无线丢包场景下，使用可复现的Docker测试平台进行验证：关键订阅者的尾部延迟（tail latency）从最高15秒降低至p95低于2毫秒；无论干扰严重程度如何，发布者吞吐量始终保持在30 Hz。

Conclusion: Adaptive Bridge通过代理解耦和自适应流量控制，有效消除了DDS背压对关键节点的级联影响，在不可靠网络环境下为ROS 2系统提供了确定性的延迟保障和吞吐量稳定性，适用于安全关键型机器人应用。

Abstract: In ROS 2 systems using DDS, a single slow subscriber on a RELIABLE topic can cause backpressure that degrades throughput and latency for all subscribers sharing the same publisher, including safety-critical local nodes. We present Adaptive Bridge, a proxy-based decoupling layer that isolates critical subscribers from noncritical ones through topic splitting and adaptive rate control. The proxy subscribes to the original topic and republishes onto two independent DDS writers, one RELIABLE for critical consumers and one BEST_EFFORT for noncritical consumers, breaking the causal chain of backpressure propagation. A probe-based classifier monitors subscriber health with hysteresis and adjusts noncritical rate limits in real time. We evaluate the system under Gilbert-Elliot bursty wireless loss using a reproducible Docker-based harness. Results show the bridge reduces critical subscriber tail latency from up to 15 seconds to under 2 milliseconds at p95 and preserves publisher throughput at 30 Hz regardless of impairment severity.

</details>


### [94] [Maintaining IoT Device Identification under Concept Drift via Budget-Aware Traffic Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15465)
*Shayan Azizi,Norihiro Okui,Masataka Nakahara,Ayumu Kubota,Gustavo Batista,Hassan Habibi Gharakaheili*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该研究针对IoT设备类型分类中的概念漂移问题，提出了一种将漂移检测与样本选择分离的策略：使用一致性漂移检测器确定需要标记的流量数量，同时采用均匀采样获取更具代表性的行为变化样本，从而在两年纵向研究中有效维持了分类器性能并提供了特征级解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于机器学习的IoT设备类型分类器在设备行为演化时会出现概念漂移，导致性能逐渐下降。现有方法需要定期使用新标记的部署流量进行重训练，但如何确定需要标记多少部署流量以及选择哪些实例进行标记，这一操作挑战尚未得到有效解决。研究者发现这两个决策应该分开处理，而非简单依赖漂移检测器选择样本。

Method: (1) 进行了为期两年的IoT流量纵向研究，分析设备行为演化在不同设备类别中的表现以及重训练如何恢复分类性能；(2) 开发了一种基于一致性(conformity)的漂移检测器，直接从原始流量特征中捕获类条件行为模型，并提供行为演化的特征级解释；(3) 提出将漂移检测确定的标记率与均匀流量采样相结合的策略，并与检测器引导的样本选择和基于置信度的自适应方法进行比较评估。研究使用了380万条IPFIX流记录，涵盖21种IoT设备类型，时间跨度超过2年。

Result: 研究表明：(1) 仅使用漂移检测器选择的实例进行重训练容易系统性地忽略新兴行为空间的部分内容，而均匀采样的部署流量能捕获更具代表性的行为变化；(2) 漂移检测在确定应标记的部署流量数量方面更为有效；(3) 将观察到的行为演化调整流量标记率与均匀流量采样相结合的策略，比检测器引导的样本选择更有效地维持分类器性能，且有利于管理流量标记工作量；(4) 该策略性能与基于置信度的自适应方法相当，同时提供了特征级解释能力。

Conclusion: 该论文的核心结论是：在IoT设备类型分类的概念漂移管理中，"标记多少"和"标记哪些"这两个决策应该分离处理。漂移检测更适合用于确定标记数量（标记率），而均匀采样更适合用于选择具体样本。这种组合策略在维持分类性能的同时，能够有效管理标记成本，并提供可解释的特征级行为演化分析，为企业和ISP网络中的IoT安全管理提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Identification of IoT device types from passive traffic is increasingly used for security management in enterprise and ISP networks. However, the performance of machine learning-based classifiers gradually degrades under concept drift as device behavior evolves. Therefore, maintaining classification performance requires periodic retraining with newly labeled deployment traffic. The operational challenge is determining how much and which deployment traffic instances to label for maintaining classification performance. We show that these two decisions should be treated separately. While retraining solely on instances selected by a drift detector is prone to systematically overlooking parts of the emerging behavioral space, uniformly sampled deployment traffic captures more representative behavioral changes. Instead, drift detection is more effective at determining the amount of deployment traffic that should be labeled. We make three contributions. (1) We conduct a two-year longitudinal study of IoT traffic and characterize how behavioral evolution manifests across device classes and how retraining with newly labeled traffic restores classification performance. (2) We develop a conformity-based drift detector that captures class-conditional behavioral models directly from raw traffic features and provides feature-level explanations of behavioral evolution. (3) We demonstrate that adjusting the traffic labeling rate according to the observed behavioral evolution, combined with uniform traffic sampling, maintains classifier performance more effectively than detector-guided sample selection and is beneficial to managing the traffic labeling effort. We further show that this strategy performs comparably to confidence-guided adaptation while providing feature-level explanations. Our evaluation uses 3.8 million IPFIX flow records collected from 21 IoT types over more than 2 years.

</details>


### [95] [WiFiSpectralJam: A Large-Scale Open Wi-Fi Spectral Scan Dataset with Controlled RF Jamming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15728)
*Dania Herzalla,Govind Singh,Willian T. Lunardi,Martin Andreoni*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: WiFiSpectralJam是一个14.52 GB的大规模Wi-Fi频谱扫描数据集，基于Raspberry Pi和Qualcomm Atheros商用硬件采集，包含52万+频谱观测，覆盖2.4/5 GHz频段的benign场景和受控RF干扰场景，支持干扰检测、频谱监测和机器学习研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏基于商用Wi-Fi硬件的大规模频谱扫描数据集，特别是在射频干扰（如干扰攻击）场景下的标准化数据资源，限制了RF干扰表征、干扰检测和频谱监测等领域的研究进展。需要构建一个公开可用的、基于廉价商用NIC（网络接口卡）的频谱数据集，以支持机器学习研究和分布偏移评估等应用。

Method: 使用Raspberry Pi Compute Module 4配合Qualcomm Atheros QCA9880 802.11ac网卡，通过Linux ath10k驱动提供的spectral-scan接口采集频谱数据。数据集涵盖主动扫描和被动扫描两种模式，覆盖2.4 GHz和5 GHz频段。数据类型包括：真实环境 benign 背景采集、RF屏蔽室 benign 本底采集，以及使用HackRF One生成的受控RF干扰采集。干扰条件按发射功率、目标信道以及主动子集中的波形类型进行变化。同时提供原始频谱记录、文件级元数据清单、派生的频谱摘要特征、验证输出和可复现的基准协议。

Result: 构建了WiFiSpectralJam数据集，包含14.52 GB数据、96,090个CSV文件、522,771,130条有序频谱观测记录。该数据集是首个基于商用Wi-Fi感知硬件的大规模频谱扫描数据集之一，支持多种应用场景：RF干扰表征、干扰检测、频谱监测、分布偏移评估，以及基于商用NIC频谱测量的机器学习研究。数据集已公开发布于Kaggle平台。

Conclusion: WiFiSpectralJam为射频干扰研究和基于机器学习的频谱分析提供了一个大规模、多场景、可复现的基准数据集。通过利用廉价商用硬件（Raspberry Pi + Qualcomm Atheros网卡），该工作降低了频谱感知研究的门槛，促进了RF干扰检测和频谱监测领域的可重复性研究。派生特征、验证输出和基准协议的提供进一步增强了数据集的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: WiFiSpectralJam is a Wi-Fi spectral-scan dataset comprising 14.52 GB, 96,090 CSV files, and 522,771,130 ordered spectral observations using commodity Wi-Fi sensing hardware. Measurements were acquired with a Raspberry Pi Compute Module 4 equipped with a Qualcomm Atheros QCA9880 802.11ac network interface and the Linux ath10k spectral-scan interface. The dataset spans active and passive scan modalities across the 2.4 and 5 GHz bands and includes real-world benign background captures, benign RF-chamber floor captures, and controlled RF-jamming captures generated with a HackRF One. Jamming conditions vary by transmit power, target channel, and, in the active subset, waveform type. The release provides the raw spectral-scan records together with a file-level metadata manifest, derived spectral-summary features, validation outputs, and reproducible benchmark protocols. These resources support reuse in RF interference characterisation, jamming detection, spectrum monitoring, distribution-shift evaluation, and machine-learning studies using commodity-NIC spectral measurements. The dataset is publicly available at: https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/daniaherzalla/radio-frequency-jamming/data.

</details>


### [96] [Scaling the Lightning Network with Practical Set Reconciliation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15921)
*Xingyu Chen,Anish Sinha,David Starobinski,Ari Trachtenberg*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文指出闪电网络的泛洪式gossip拓扑同步机制效率低下，提出采用集合和解协议替代，并设计了自适应IBLT协议ADAPTIVEIBLT，将同步时间从数小时缩短至数分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闪电网络(LN)目前依赖泛洪式gossip协议在节点间共享网络拓扑、通道公告与更新以及节点公告信息，但这种机制存在根本性的效率问题，导致拓扑同步耗时过长，难以适应网络规模扩展的需求。

Method: 作者首先对现有集合和解协议在真实网络条件下进行系统性评估；随后提出ADAPTIVEIBLT——一种具有部分解码增强功能的自适应IBLT（可逆布隆查找表）协议；最后通过在Core-Lightning中模拟和解过程并评估真实gossip快照来验证方案。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于集合和解的方法能够显著优化gossip同步效率，将拓扑和解时间从数小时量级降低至数分钟，证明了该方案在实际闪电网络环境中具备良好的可扩展性和实用价值。

Conclusion: 泛洪式gossip和解在闪电网络中本质上效率低下，而基于集合和解的协议（特别是提出的ADAPTIVEIBLT）能够有效解决这一问题，大幅提升闪电网络拓扑信息的同步速度，为闪电网络的规模化发展提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: The Lightning Network (LN) utilizes gossip to share network topology, channel announcements and updates, and node announcements among its local constituents. Yet, our measurements show that this flooding-based gossip reconciliation is fundamentally inefficient. We propose, instead, to use set reconciliation protocols for sharing this information, and we systematically evaluate existing approaches under realistic network conditions. We further propose ADAPTIVEIBLT, a novel adaptive IBLT (Invertible Bloom Lookup Table) protocol with a partial-decoding enhancement. By simulating reconciliation in Core-Lightning and evaluating real gossip snapshots, we demonstrate the practical benefits of reconciliation in scaling gossip reconciliation from hours down to a few minutes.

</details>


### [97] [SbDN: Source-based TSN-Grade Deterministic Networking using Commodity Switches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16199)
*Mohammadparsa Karimi,Majid Nabi,Andrew Nelson,Kees Goossens,Twan Basten*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: SbDN是一种多智能体源架构，通过将调度智能集中到控制器并在源端点强制执行配置，利用商用以太网交换机实现TSN级确定性。提出的TNP和TP两种方法均经过形式化验证，在40种基准测试中实现100%时间敏感流量准入，以更低成本提供优于TSN的延迟性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性网络对于汽车、工业和航空航天系统中的安全关键应用至关重要，这些应用需要为时间敏感流量保证有界的端到端延迟。TSN虽然能提供此类保证，但其部署需要在每个跳点使用昂贵的TSN兼容交换机，并且需要复杂的逐交换机配置，这阻碍了运行时重配置。因此，需要一种既能实现TSN级确定性、又能降低基础设施成本并支持运行时重配置的替代方案。

Method: 本文提出了SbDN（基于源的多智能体架构），一种将调度智能全部移至由三个协作智能体组成的集中式控制器，并仅在源端点强制执行计算配置的多智能体源架构，使交换机保持为简单的转发元素。提出了两种方法：（1）时间网络分区（TNP）——在纯FIFO交换机上提供严格的时间隔离；（2）流量优先级（TP）——利用交换机上的严格优先级队列，支持工作守恒尽力而为流量。两种方法均经过形式化证明，确保所有准入的时间敏感流满足端到端截止时间。

Result: 在两种拓扑结构的40种基准配置上的评估表明：TNP和TP在每个场景中都实现了100%的时间敏感流量准入率，调度时间处于低毫秒范围，适用于安全的运行时重配置。与标准TSN基线相比，SbDN以极低的交换机基础设施成本提供了更优的时间敏感延迟，同时通过两种方法的选择提供了具有竞争力的尽力而为吞吐量。

Conclusion: SbDN通过将调度智能从交换机转移至源端，成功在商用以太网交换机上实现了TSN级确定性，克服了传统TSN部署成本高和配置复杂的痛点。TNP和TP两种方法分别适用于不同的应用场景需求，且均具备形式化保证和高效的运行时重配置能力，为安全关键系统中的确定性网络提供了一种经济高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Deterministic networking is essential for safety-critical applications in automotive, industrial, and aerospace systems, where bounded end-to-end latency must be guaranteed for time-critical traffic. Time-Sensitive Networking (TSN) provides the mechanisms to achieve such guarantees, but its deployment requires expensive TSN-capable switches at every hop and complex per-switch configuration that hinders runtime reconfiguration. This paper presents SbDN, a Multi-Agent Source-based architecture that achieves TSN-grade determinism using commodity Ethernet switches. SbDN moves all scheduling intelligence to a centralized controller composed of three cooperating agents and enforces the computed configurations exclusively at the source endpoints, leaving switches as simple forwarding elements. We propose two methods: Temporal Network Partitioning (TNP), which provides strict temporal isolation on pure FIFO switches, and Traffic Prioritization (TP), which leverages strict-priority queuing at switches to enable work-conserving best-effort traffic. Both methods are formally proven to guarantee that all admitted time-critical flows meet their end-to-end deadlines. Evaluation across 40 benchmark configurations on two topologies shows that TNP and TP achieve 100\% admission of time-critical traffic in every scenario, with scheduling times in the low-millisecond range suitable for safe runtime reconfiguration. Compared to a standard TSN baseline, SbDN delivers superior time-critical latency at a fraction of the switch infrastructure cost, while offering competitive best-effort throughput through the choice between the two methods.

</details>


### [98] [Towards the Interplanetary Internet: An IoT Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16330)
*Carles Gomez,Jon Crowcroft*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文论证了IP-based IoT协议适用于星际互联网，通过对比深空与IoT场景的相似性并结合IETF标准，展望了深空IP通信的未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共管理部门和私营企业已宣布计划部署网络基础设施，以支持未来在月球、火星等深空目标附近或表面的机器人和人类活动。尽管此前深空通信采用IP协议栈曾被忽视，但近期事件促使人们重新考虑使用IP来构建星际互联网（Interplanetary Internet）。

Method: 本文通过对比分析深空场景与物联网（IoT）场景的相似性，梳理IETF相关标准化工作，探讨IP-based IoT协议在深空环境中的适用性。

Result: 论证了IP-based IoT协议在星际互联网中的可行性，明确了现有IETF标准在深空通信中的整合潜力，并指出了该领域的机遇与未来发展方向。

Conclusion: 深空环境与IoT场景具有高度相似性（如高延迟、间歇性连接、资源受限等），IP-based IoT协议有望成为星际互联网的重要技术基础；未来需持续推进IETF标准化工作，以支持深空环境下的可靠通信。

Abstract: Public administrations and private companies have announced plans to deploy networking infrastructure to support future robotic and human presence on or near space targets, such as the Moon and Mars. While using an IP protocol stack for deepspace communication had been neglected, recent events have motivated the reconsideration of IP to enable the Interplanetary Internet. This new paradigm facilitates the integration of IP-based Internet of Things (IoT) protocols for deep-space environments. This paper illustrates the similarities between deep-space and IoT scenarios, presents related IETF standardization work, and discusses opportunities and future directions for IP-based IoT protocols in the Interplanetary Internet.

</details>


### [99] [OVS Meets PQ-TLS: Exploring Post-Quantum TLS for SDN's Southbound API](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16582)
*Majd Latah,Kubra Kalkan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了后量子TLS在SDN南向API中的性能表现，通过概念验证实现对比分析了纯/混合PQ-TLS与传统TLS的延迟和CPU利用率，为SDN的抗量子安全迁移提供了性能基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件定义网络（SDN）的南向API目前依赖使用传统密码算法（如RSA和ECDSA）的TLS协议，随着量子计算的发展，这些传统密码算法面临被量子计算机破解的风险，因此需要研究后量子密码学在SDN南向API中的应用可行性。

Method: 本文提出了在SDN南向API中使用后量子TLS（PQ-TLS）的概念验证实现，研究了纯后量子和混合PQ-TLS模式在不同安全级别下的性能表现，并与传统TLS进行了对比分析，同时比较了不同的后量子签名和密钥协商方案的性能。

Result: 论文分析了PQ-TLS在SDN南向API中的延迟和CPU利用率性能，对比了纯PQ-TLS模式、混合PQ-TLS模式与传统TLS在不同安全级别下的性能差异，并评估了不同后量子签名算法和密钥建立方案的性能表现。

Conclusion: 该研究为在SDN南向API中部署后量子密码学提供了性能基准和可行性分析，证明了PQ-TLS在SDN环境中的实用性，为未来网络基础设施的抗量子安全迁移提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Software-defined networking (SDN) is a novel networking paradigm that enables network programmability and centralized control for network devices. The southbound application programming interface (API) is used to control and manage the underlying data plane devices. The existing southbound API relies on TLS with legacy cryptographic algorithms such as RSA and ECDSA. In this paper, we explore the performance of the southbound API with postquantum TLS (PQ-TLS) support. We present a proof-of-concept of using PQ-TLS in SDN's southbound API. We study the performance of pure and hybrid PQ-TLS modes across different security levels and compare them with legacy TLS in terms of latency and CPU utilization. We also compare the performance of different post-quantum signature and key establishment schemes.

</details>


### [100] [Expanding Access, Exposing Risk: A Short Study of Exposed Starlink Hosts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16839)
*Omar Elamri,Isaac-Neil Zanoria,Jacob Zhi,Ben Du,Liz Izhikevich*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过测量分析发现Starlink主机比非Starlink主机更容易运行过时/漏洞系统，且拉丁美洲、东南亚和东欧地区风险更高，揭示了卫星互联网安全态势的区域差异性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Starlink卫星互联网服务的快速部署，其用户规模不断扩大，但关于Starlink连接主机的安全特性尚缺乏系统性研究。作者希望通过大规模测量分析，揭示Starlink网络生态系统的安全态势，为互联网测量研究和政策制定提供数据支撑。

Method: 采用测量驱动（measurement-driven）的研究方法，对Starlink连接主机进行大规模安全特征分析。通过主动/被动测量技术收集Starlink主机的操作系统版本、网络协议版本等安全相关数据，并与非Starlink主机进行对比分析，同时按地理区域进行分层统计。

Result: 研究发现Starlink主机相比非Starlink主机更可能运行过时或存在漏洞的操作系统和网络协议。拉丁美洲、东南亚和东欧等地区的安全风险呈现不成比例的高风险特征，表明这些区域的Starlink用户面临更大的网络安全威胁。

Conclusion: Starlink网络的安全态势存在显著的区域不均衡性，部分发展中地区的用户面临更高的安全风险。这些发现对互联网测量社区和政策制定者提出了重要问题，需要关注卫星互联网普及带来的安全挑战，并考虑制定相应的安全标准和监管措施。

Abstract: In this very short paper, we present a measurement-driven analysis of the security characteristics of Starlink-connected hosts and uncover several concerning trends. We find that Starlink hosts are more likely to run outdated or vulnerable operating systems and network protocols than non-Starlink hosts. Regions like Latin America, Southeast Asia, and Eastern Europe show disproportionately higher risk. Our findings raise important questions for the Internet measurement and policy communities.

</details>
